          .trim()
          .toLowerCase() === targetLower,
    )
    .sort((a, b) => compareByPriorityOnly(a, b));

  activeQuickPriorityInitial = new Set();
  activeQuickPriorityItems.forEach((item) => {
    if (Number(item.priority) >= 1) {
      activeQuickPriorityInitial.add(String(item.id));
    }
  });
  activeQuickPriorityDraft = new Set(activeQuickPriorityInitial);

  document.getElementById("quick-priority-subtitle").textContent = `ðŸ¢ ${
    activeQuickPriorityOffice.office
  }${cleanPin ? ` â€¢ ðŸ“ ${cleanPin}` : ""}`;

  const filterInput = document.getElementById("quick-priority-filter-input");
  if (filterInput) filterInput.value = "";

  const saveBtn = document.getElementById("quick-priority-save-btn");
  saveBtn.classList.remove("is-loading", "is-success");

  renderQuickPriorityChips();

  const overlay = document.getElementById("quick-priority-modal-overlay");
  const chipsContainer = document.getElementById(
    "quick-priority-chips-container",
  );
  overlay.classList.add("show");
  if (chipsContainer) chipsContainer.scrollTop = 0;
  overlay.scrollTop = 0;
}

function closeQuickPriorityModal() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById("quick-priority-modal-overlay");
  const chipsContainer = document.getElementById(
    "quick-priority-chips-container",
  );
  overlay?.classList.remove("show");
  if (chipsContainer) chipsContainer.scrollTop = 0;
  if (overlay) overlay.scrollTop = 0;
}

function toggleQuickPriorityNonBoOnly() {
  quickPriorityNonBoOnly = !quickPriorityNonBoOnly;
  renderQuickPriorityChips();
}

function renderQuickPriorityChips() {
  const container = document.getElementById("quick-priority-chips-container");
  if (!container) return;

  const q = (
    document.getElementById("quick-priority-filter-input")?.value || ""
  )
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();

  const visibleItems = activeQuickPriorityItems.filter((item) => {
    const ot = detectOfficeType(item.Address);
    if (quickPriorityNonBoOnly && ot) return false;
    if (
      q &&
      !String(item.Address || "")
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(q)
    )
      return false;
    return true;
  });

  if (visibleItems.length === 0) {
    container.innerHTML = `<div style="width: 100%; text-align: center; padding: 18px 8px; font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-light);">No matching addresses found.</div>`;
  } else {
    container.innerHTML = visibleItems
      .map((item) => {
        const idStr = String(item.id);
        const ot = detectOfficeType(item.Address);
        const isAutoTop = ot === "HO" || ot === "SO";
        const isSelected = activeQuickPriorityDraft.has(idStr);

        if (isAutoTop) {
          return `
            <button type="button" class="priority-select-chip is-auto-top" disabled title="Offices always rank on top automatically">
              <span>ðŸ“® ${escapeHTML(item.Address)}</span>
              <span class="priority-chip-tag">Auto-Top</span>
            </button>`;
        }

        return `
  <button
    type="button"
    class="priority-select-chip ${isSelected ? "selected" : ""}"
    onpointerdown="event.preventDefault()"
    onclick="toggleQuickPriorityItem('${escapeForOnclick(idStr)}', this)"
  >
    <span>${isSelected ? "âš¡" : ot === "BO" ? "ðŸ“®" : "â€¢"}</span>
    <span>${escapeHTML(item.Address)}</span>
    ${ot === "BO" ? `<span class="priority-chip-tag">Office</span>` : ""}
  </button>`;
      })
      .join("");
  }

  updateQuickPriorityUI();
}

function toggleQuickPriorityItem(idStr, chipEl) {
  const item = activeQuickPriorityItems.find((i) => String(i.id) === idStr);
  const ot = item ? detectOfficeType(item.Address) : null;

  if (activeQuickPriorityDraft.has(idStr)) {
    activeQuickPriorityDraft.delete(idStr);
    chipEl?.classList.remove("selected");
    const iconSpan = chipEl?.querySelector("span");
    if (iconSpan) iconSpan.textContent = ot === "BO" ? "ðŸ“®" : "â€¢";
  } else {
    activeQuickPriorityDraft.add(idStr);
    chipEl?.classList.add("selected");
    const iconSpan = chipEl?.querySelector("span");
    if (iconSpan) iconSpan.textContent = "âš¡";
  }

  if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(20);
  updateQuickPriorityUI();
}

function updateQuickPriorityUI() {
  const countEl = document.getElementById("quick-priority-count");
  const hideBoBtn = document.getElementById("quick-priority-hide-bo-btn");
  const saveBtn = document.getElementById("quick-priority-save-btn");
  const saveBtnContent = document.getElementById(
    "quick-priority-save-btn-content",
  );

  const { totalChanges } = getQuickPriorityDelta();

  if (countEl) {
    countEl.textContent = `${activeQuickPriorityDraft.size} marked Priority`;
    countEl.style.color =
      activeQuickPriorityDraft.size > 0 ? "#d97706" : "var(--text-light)";
  }

  if (hideBoBtn) {
    hideBoBtn.textContent = quickPriorityNonBoOnly
      ? "Show All"
      : "Hide Offices";
  }

  if (
    saveBtn &&
    !saveBtn.classList.contains("is-loading") &&
    !saveBtn.classList.contains("is-success")
  ) {
    saveBtn.disabled = totalChanges === 0;
    saveBtnContent.innerHTML = getQuickPrioritySaveBtnHTML(totalChanges);
  }
}

// In saveQuickPriorityModal() â€” lock button BEFORE ensureOnline() and verify v_updated
async function saveQuickPriorityModal() {
  const { promoteIds, demoteIds, totalChanges } = getQuickPriorityDelta();
  if (totalChanges === 0) return;

  const saveBtn = document.getElementById("quick-priority-save-btn");
  const saveBtnContent = document.getElementById(
    "quick-priority-save-btn-content",
  );

  // Lock immediately to prevent taps during ensureOnline()
  saveBtn.classList.add("is-loading");
  saveBtn.disabled = true;
  saveBtnContent.innerHTML = `<div class="btn-spinner"></div><span>Saving...</span>`;

  if (!(await ensureOnline("Connect to internet to save priority changes."))) {
    saveBtn.classList.remove("is-loading");
    saveBtn.disabled = false;
    saveBtnContent.innerHTML = getQuickPrioritySaveBtnHTML(totalChanges);
    return;
  }

  try {
    const { data: updatedCount, error } = await supabaseClient.rpc(
      "update_address_priorities",
      {
        promote_ids: promoteIds,
        demote_ids: demoteIds,
      },
    );
    if (error) throw error;

    // Update local cache
    const promoteSet = new Set(promoteIds.map(String));
    const demoteSet = new Set(demoteIds.map(String));

    cachedPostOffices.forEach((row) => {
      const idStr = String(row.id);
      if (promoteSet.has(idStr)) {
        row.priority = 1;
        updateSearchIndexRow(row);
      } else if (demoteSet.has(idStr)) {
        row.priority = 0;
        updateSearchIndexRow(row);
      }
    });

    persistPostOfficeCache();
    refreshSearchInPlace();
    refreshDirectoryInPlace();

    if (typeof updatedCount === "number" && updatedCount !== totalChanges) {
      showToast(
        `âš ï¸ Server updated ${updatedCount} of ${totalChanges}. Syncing latest state...`,
        "warning",
      );
      await fetchAllPostOffices(true);
    } else {
      showToast(
        `âš¡ Updated ${totalChanges} ${
          totalChanges === 1 ? "address" : "addresses"
        } for all staff!`,
      );
    }

    saveBtn.classList.remove("is-loading");
    saveBtn.classList.add("is-success");
    saveBtnContent.innerHTML = `
      <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
        <polyline class="checkmark-path" points="20 6 9 17 4 12"></polyline>
      </svg>
      <span>Saved!</span>
    `;

    setTimeout(() => {
      closeQuickPriorityModal();
    }, 600);
  } catch (err) {
    saveBtn.classList.remove("is-loading", "is-success");
    saveBtn.disabled = false;
    saveBtnContent.innerHTML = getQuickPrioritySaveBtnHTML(totalChanges);
    alert("Error updating priorities: " + err.message);
  }
}


// ===== FILE: 08-records-upload.js =====
// =========================================================
// 08-RECORDS-UPLOAD.JS â€” Edit, Delete, History, Manual/Bulk & Excel

// --- IN-PLACE EDIT RECORD MODAL ---
let activeInlineEditRecord = null;

function closeEditRecordModal() {
  document.getElementById("edit-record-modal-overlay").classList.remove("show");
  closeAllDropdowns();
  activeInlineEditRecord = null;
}

function getInlineSaveBtnDefaultHTML() {
  const label = isCurrentUserAdmin ? "Save Changes" : "Submit Edit";
  return `
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
      <path d="M19 21H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h11l5 5v11a2 2 0 0 1-2 2z"></path>
      <polyline points="17 21 17 13 7 13 7 21"></polyline>
      <polyline points="7 3 7 8 15 8"></polyline>
    </svg>
    <span>${label}</span>
  `;
}

function updateRecord(id, currentAddress, currentOffice, currentPin) {
  closeAllDirectoryActionTrays();

  const safePin =
    !currentPin || currentPin === "null" || currentPin === "undefined"
      ? ""
      : String(currentPin).trim();

  activeInlineEditRecord = {
    id: id,
    oldAddress: currentAddress || "",
    oldOffice: currentOffice || "",
    oldPin: safePin,
  };

  const addrInput = document.getElementById("inline-edit-address");
  const offInput = document.getElementById("inline-edit-office");
  const pinInput = document.getElementById("inline-edit-pin");
  const clearOffBtn = document.getElementById("inline-edit-office-clear");
  const saveBtn = document.getElementById("inline-edit-save-btn");
  const saveBtnContent = document.getElementById(
    "inline-edit-save-btn-content",
  );

  addrInput.value = activeInlineEditRecord.oldAddress;
  offInput.value = activeInlineEditRecord.oldOffice;
  pinInput.value = activeInlineEditRecord.oldPin;

  if (offInput.value.length > 0) clearOffBtn.classList.remove("hidden");
  else clearOffBtn.classList.add("hidden");

  saveBtn.classList.remove("is-loading", "is-success");
  saveBtn.disabled = false;
  saveBtnContent.innerHTML = getInlineSaveBtnDefaultHTML();

  document.getElementById("edit-record-modal-overlay").classList.add("show");
  setTimeout(() => addrInput.focus(), 140);
}

function applyLocalRecordEdit(id, newAddress, newOffice, newPin) {
  upsertLocalPostOffice({
    id: id,
    Address: newAddress,
    Office: newOffice,
    Pin: newPin || null,
  });
}

async function saveInlineEditRecord() {
  if (!(await ensureOnline("Connect to internet to save changes."))) return;
  if (!activeInlineEditRecord) return;

  const newAddress = document
    .getElementById("inline-edit-address")
    .value.trim();
  const newOffice = document.getElementById("inline-edit-office").value.trim();
  const newPin = document.getElementById("inline-edit-pin").value.trim();

  if (!newAddress || !newOffice) {
    return showToast("âš ï¸ Address and Office are required.");
  }
  if (isSelfParentOfficeRow(newAddress, newOffice)) {
    return showToast(
      "â„¹ï¸ Parent HO/SO is already shown as the office header. Tap ðŸ‘¥ Staff there instead.",
    );
  }

  if (
    newAddress === activeInlineEditRecord.oldAddress &&
    newOffice === activeInlineEditRecord.oldOffice &&
    newPin === activeInlineEditRecord.oldPin
  ) {
    closeEditRecordModal();
    return showToast("No changes made.");
  }

  const {
    data: { session },
  } = await supabaseClient.auth.getSession();

  if (!session || !session.user) {
    closeEditRecordModal();
    checkUserSession();
    return alert("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  const saveBtn = document.getElementById("inline-edit-save-btn");
  const saveBtnContent = document.getElementById(
    "inline-edit-save-btn-content",
  );
  const editedId = activeInlineEditRecord.id;

  saveBtn.classList.add("is-loading");
  saveBtn.disabled = true;
  saveBtnContent.innerHTML = `
    <div class="btn-spinner"></div>
    <span>Saving...</span>
  `;

  const reqData = {
    post_office_id: editedId,
    action_type: "EDIT",
    proposed_data: {
      Address: newAddress,
      Office: newOffice,
      Pin: newPin || null,
    },
    original_data: {
      Address: activeInlineEditRecord.oldAddress,
      Office: activeInlineEditRecord.oldOffice,
      Pin: activeInlineEditRecord.oldPin || null,
    },
    requested_by: session.user.id,
  };

  try {
    if (isCurrentUserAdmin) {
      const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc("admin_direct_submit", {
        request_payloads: [reqData],
      });
      if (error) throw error;

      applyLocalRecordEdit(editedId, newAddress, newOffice, newPin);
      invalidateApprovalsCache();

      saveBtn.classList.remove("is-loading");
      saveBtn.classList.add("is-success");
      saveBtnContent.innerHTML = `
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
          <polyline class="checkmark-path" points="20 6 9 17 4 12"></polyline>
        </svg>
        <span>Saved!</span>
      `;
      showToast("âœ¨ Changes saved instantly!");

      setTimeout(() => {
        closeEditRecordModal();
        highlightUpdatedRecord(editedId);
      }, 650);
    } else {
      const { error } = await supabaseClient
        .from("edit_requests")
        .insert([reqData]);
      if (error) throw error;

      invalidateApprovalsCache();

      saveBtn.classList.remove("is-loading");
      saveBtn.classList.add("is-success");
      saveBtnContent.innerHTML = `
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
          <polyline class="checkmark-path" points="20 6 9 17 4 12"></polyline>
        </svg>
        <span>Submitted!</span>
      `;
      showToast("âœ… Edit request submitted for approval!");

      setTimeout(() => {
        closeEditRecordModal();
      }, 650);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    saveBtn.classList.remove("is-loading", "is-success");
    saveBtn.disabled = false;
    saveBtnContent.innerHTML = getInlineSaveBtnDefaultHTML();
    alert("Error saving edit: " + err.message);
  }
}

function highlightUpdatedRecord(id) {
  const safeId = CSS.escape(String(id));
  document.querySelectorAll(`[data-record-id="${safeId}"]`).forEach((el) => {
    el.classList.remove("record-updated-flash");
    void el.offsetWidth;
    el.classList.add("record-updated-flash");
    setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove("record-updated-flash"), 1200);
  });
}

document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
  if (
    e.key === "Escape" &&
    document
      .getElementById("edit-record-modal-overlay")
      ?.classList.contains("show")
  ) {
    closeEditRecordModal();
  }
});

// --- DELETE SINGLE RECORD ---
function deleteRecord(id) {
  let title = "Request Deletion?";
  let message =
    "This will submit a deletion request to the community for approval. Continue?";
  let btnText = "Request Delete";

  if (isCurrentUserAdmin) {
    title = "Admin: Force Delete?";
    message =
      "As an Admin, this deletion will be applied immediately without requiring community votes. Continue?";
    btnText = "Delete Instantly";
  }

  showCustomConfirm(title, message, btnText, async () => {
    if (!(await ensureOnline("Connect to internet to save changes."))) return;
    const {
      data: { session },
    } = await supabaseClient.auth.getSession();

    if (!session || !session.user) {
      checkUserSession();
      return alert("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
    }

    const reqData = {
      post_office_id: id,
      action_type: "DELETE",
      proposed_data: {},
      requested_by: session.user.id,
    };

    if (isCurrentUserAdmin) {
      try {
        const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc("admin_direct_submit", {
          request_payloads: [reqData],
        });
        if (error) throw error;
        removeLocalPostOffice(id);
        closeAllDirectoryActionTrays();
        showToast("ðŸ—‘ï¸ Admin: Record deleted instantly.");
      } catch (err) {
        alert("Error: " + err.message);
      }
    } else {
      const { error } = await supabaseClient
        .from("edit_requests")
        .insert(reqData);
      if (error) alert("Error: " + error.message);
      else showToast("ðŸ“ Deletion request submitted for approval.");
    }
    invalidateApprovalsCache();
  });
}

// --- RECORD HISTORY AUDIT MODAL ---
function closeHistoryModal() {
  document.getElementById("history-modal-overlay").classList.remove("show");
}

async function viewHistory(postOfficeId) {
  closeAllDirectoryActionTrays();
  const contentDiv = document.getElementById("history-modal-content");
  contentDiv.innerHTML = `<ul class="list-container"><li class="list-empty">Fetching records...</li></ul>`;
  document.getElementById("history-modal-overlay").classList.add("show");

  const showCachedHistoryFallback = () => {
    showOfflineToast("Showing cached history.");
    const savedHistory = localStorage.getItem(
      "dakmitra_history_" + postOfficeId,
    );
    contentDiv.innerHTML =
      savedHistory ||
      `<ul class="list-container"><li class="list-empty">ðŸ“´ No internet connection. No cached history for this record.</li></ul>`;
  };

  if (!navigator.onLine) {
    showCachedHistoryFallback();
    return;
  }

  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabaseClient
      .from("post_offices_history")
      .select("*")
      .eq("post_office_id", postOfficeId)
      .order("changed_at", { ascending: false });

    if (error) throw error;

    if (!data || data.length === 0) {
      contentDiv.innerHTML = `<ul class="list-container"><li class="list-empty">No history recorded for this entry yet.</li></ul>`;
      return;
    }

    // Collect all unique user IDs and resolve via SECURITY DEFINER RPC (RLS-safe)
    const userIdsToFetch = new Set();
    data.forEach((log) => {
      if (log.changed_by) userIdsToFetch.add(log.changed_by);
      if (log.approved_by) userIdsToFetch.add(log.approved_by);
    });

    const profilesMap = {};
    if (userIdsToFetch.size > 0) {
      const { data: profileData, error: profileErr } = await supabaseClient.rpc(
        "get_public_profiles_by_ids",
        {
          user_ids: Array.from(userIdsToFetch),
        },
      );
      if (profileErr) throw profileErr;
      if (Array.isArray(profileData)) {
        profileData.forEach((p) => {
          profilesMap[p.id] = p;
        });
      }
    }

    let html = `<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px; margin-top: 10px;">`;

    data.forEach((log) => {
      const dateStr = new Date(log.changed_at).toLocaleString("en-IN", {
        dateStyle: "medium",
        timeStyle: "short",
      });

      const reqProfile = log.changed_by ? profilesMap[log.changed_by] : null;
      const appProfile = log.approved_by ? profilesMap[log.approved_by] : null;

      const requesterName = reqProfile?.name || "System / Direct Upload";
      const requesterId = reqProfile?.employee_id || "No ID";
      const requesterLink = renderUserProfileLink(
        log.changed_by,
        requesterName,
      );

      let approverHtml = "";
      if (appProfile) {
        const approverId = appProfile.employee_id || "No ID";
        const approverLink = renderUserProfileLink(
          log.approved_by,
          appProfile.name,
        );
        if (appProfile.is_admin) {
          approverHtml = `
            <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--success-text); margin-top: 4px;">
              âœ… <b>Admin Approved</b> (Approved by: ${approverLink} - ${escapeHTML(
                approverId,
              )})
            </div>`;
        } else {
          approverHtml = `
            <div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--primary); margin-top: 4px;">
              ðŸ‘¥ <b>Community Approved</b> (Final vote by: ${approverLink} - ${escapeHTML(
                approverId,
              )})
            </div>`;
        }
      }

      let actionBadge = "";
      let changesHtml = "";

      const formatField = (
        oldVal,
        newVal,
        prefix = "",
        fallback = "N/A",
        linkTargets = null,
      ) => {
        const rawOld =
          oldVal === undefined || oldVal === null ? "" : String(oldVal).trim();
        const rawNew =
          newVal === undefined || newVal === null ? "" : String(newVal).trim();
        const sOld = rawOld === "" ? fallback : rawOld;
        const sNew = rawNew === "" ? fallback : rawNew;

        const oldHtml = linkTargets
          ? renderDirectoryLink(
              sOld,
              linkTargets.oldOffice,
              linkTargets.oldPin,
              "is-muted",
            )
          : escapeHTML(sOld);

        const newHtml = linkTargets
          ? renderDirectoryLink(sNew, linkTargets.newOffice, linkTargets.newPin)
          : escapeHTML(sNew);

        if (sOld !== sNew) {
          return `<div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">${prefix} <span style="text-decoration:line-through; color:var(--text-light);">${oldHtml}</span> âž¡ï¸ <b>${newHtml}</b></div>`;
        }
        return `<div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">${prefix} ${newHtml}</div>`;
      };

      if (log.action === "INSERT") {
        actionBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#dcfce7;color:#166534; padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 12px;">âž• CREATED</span>`;
        const newOff = log.new_data?.Office || log.new_data?.office || "";
        const newPin = log.new_data?.Pin ?? log.new_data?.pin ?? "";
        changesHtml = `
          <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;"><b>${escapeHTML(
            log.new_data?.Address,
          )}</b></div>
          <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">ðŸ¢ ${renderDirectoryLink(
            newOff || "Unknown Office",
            newOff,
            newPin,
          )}</div>
          <div>ðŸ“ ${renderDirectoryLink(newPin || "N/A", newOff, newPin)}</div>
        `;
      } else if (log.action === "UPDATE") {
        actionBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#fef9c3;color:#854d0e; padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 12px;">âœï¸ EDITED</span>`;
        const oldD = log.old_data || {};
        const newD = log.new_data || {};
        const oldOff = oldD.Office || oldD.office || "";
        const newOff = newD.Office || newD.office || "";
        const oldPin = oldD.Pin ?? oldD.pin ?? "";
        const newPin = newD.Pin ?? newD.pin ?? "";

        const linkTargets = {
          oldOffice: oldOff,
          newOffice: newOff,
          oldPin: oldPin,
          newPin: newPin,
        };

        changesHtml =
          formatField(
            oldD.Address || oldD.address,
            newD.Address || newD.address,
            "ðŸ“®",
            "Unknown Address",
          ) +
          formatField(oldOff, newOff, "ðŸ¢", "Unknown Office", linkTargets) +
          formatField(oldPin, newPin, "ðŸ“", "N/A", linkTargets);

        if (oldD.is_deleted !== newD.is_deleted) {
          changesHtml += `<div style="margin-top: 8px;"><b>Status:</b> ${
            newD.is_deleted ? "Marked as Deleted ðŸ—‘ï¸" : "Restored â™»ï¸"
          }</div>`;
        }
      } else if (log.action === "DELETE") {
        actionBadge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b; padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 12px;">ðŸ—‘ï¸ DELETED</span>`;
        const oldOff = log.old_data?.Office || log.old_data?.office || "";
        const oldPin = log.old_data?.Pin ?? log.old_data?.pin ?? "";
        changesHtml = `
          <div style="text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--text-light); margin-bottom: 4px;"><b>${escapeHTML(
            log.old_data?.Address,
          )}</b></div>
          <div style="text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--text-light); margin-bottom: 4px;">ðŸ¢ ${renderDirectoryLink(
            oldOff || "Unknown Office",
            oldOff,
            oldPin,
            "is-muted",
          )}</div>
          <div style="text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--text-light);">ðŸ“ ${renderDirectoryLink(
            oldPin || "N/A",
            oldOff,
            oldPin,
            "is-muted",
          )}</div>
          <div style="margin-top: 8px; color: #991b1b; font-weight: 600;">Record was permanently deleted.</div>
        `;
      }

      const isBulkExcelUpload =
        log.action === "INSERT" && log.new_data?.upload_method === "excel_bulk";

      const attributionHtml = isBulkExcelUpload
        ? `<div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--success-text); margin-top: 12px; padding-top: 8px; border-top: 1px dashed var(--border);">
             ðŸ“Š <b>Added via Excel Bulk Upload</b> by Admin ${requesterLink} (${escapeHTML(
               requesterId,
             )})
           </div>`
        : `<div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-light); margin-top: 12px; padding-top: 8px; border-top: 1px dashed var(--border);">
             <div>ðŸ‘¤ Requested by: ${requesterLink} (${escapeHTML(
               requesterId,
             )})</div>
             ${approverHtml}
           </div>`;

      html += `
        <div style="background: var(--btn-secondary); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 12px; padding: 12px;">
          <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 8px;">
            ${actionBadge}
            <span style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-light); font-weight: 600;">${dateStr}</span>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 8px; color: var(--text); line-height: 1.4;">
            ${changesHtml}
          </div>
          ${attributionHtml}
        </div>
      `;
    });

    html += `</div>`;
    contentDiv.innerHTML = html;
    localStorage.setItem("dakmitra_history_" + postOfficeId, html);
  } catch (err) {
    if (isNetworkError(err)) {
      showCachedHistoryFallback();
      return;
    }
    contentDiv.innerHTML = `<ul class="list-container"><li class="list-empty" style="color: red;">Error: ${escapeHTML(
      err.message,
    )}</li></ul>`;
  }
}

// --- MANUAL ENTRY ROWS ---
function getManualEntryRowHTML(showLabels = false) {
  return `
    <div class="manual-entry-row">
      <div class="input-group" style="flex: 1; margin-bottom: 0; text-align: left;">
        ${showLabels ? "<label>Address</label>" : ""}
        <input type="search" class="manual-address" placeholder="Address" autocomplete="off" />
      </div>
      <div class="input-group" style="flex: 1; margin-bottom: 0; text-align: left;">
        ${showLabels ? "<label>Office</label>" : ""}
        <div class="autocomplete-wrapper">
          <input type="search" class="manual-office" placeholder="Search & Select Office..." autocomplete="off" oninput="handleOfficeInput(this)" onfocus="handleOfficeInput(this)" onblur="scheduleCloseDropdowns(200)" style="padding-right: 40px; width: 100%;" />
          <button type="button" class="clear-btn hidden" onpointerdown="event.preventDefault()" style="position: absolute; right: 6px; top: 6px; width: 36px; height: 36px; padding: 0; margin: 0; z-index: 2;" onclick="clearOfficeInput(this)" title="Clear Office">âœ•</button>
          <div class="autocomplete-dropdown"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="input-group" style="flex: 0.7; margin-bottom: 0; text-align: left;">
        ${showLabels ? "<label>Pin</label>" : ""}
        <input type="search" inputmode="numeric" class="manual-Pin" placeholder="e.g. 231305" autocomplete="off" />
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="btn" style="width: auto; background: var(--border); color: var(--text); padding: 14px;" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()" title="Remove row">âœ•</button>
    </div>
  `;
}

(function initManualEntries() {
  const container = document.getElementById("manual-entries-container");
  if (container) container.innerHTML = getManualEntryRowHTML(true);
})();

function addInputRow() {
  document
    .getElementById("manual-entries-container")
    .insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", getManualEntryRowHTML(false));
}

async function submitMultipleRecords() {
  if (!(await ensureOnline("Connect to internet to save changes."))) return;
  const rows = document.querySelectorAll(".manual-entry-row");
  const requests = [];
  const adminDirectEdits = [];

  const {
    data: { session },
  } = await supabaseClient.auth.getSession();

  if (!session || !session.user) {
    checkUserSession();
    return alert("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  let skippedParentOffice = 0;

  rows.forEach((row) => {
    const address = row.querySelector(".manual-address").value.trim();
    const office = row.querySelector(".manual-office").value.trim();
    const Pin = row.querySelector(".manual-Pin").value.trim();
    const editId = row.getAttribute("data-edit-id");

    if (address && office) {
      if (isSelfParentOfficeRow(address, office)) {
        skippedParentOffice++;
        return;
      }
      if (editId) {
        const oldAddr = row.getAttribute("data-old-addr");
        const oldOff = row.getAttribute("data-old-off");
        const oldPin = row.getAttribute("data-old-pin");

        const reqData = {
          post_office_id: editId,
          action_type: "EDIT",
          proposed_data: {
            Address: address,
            Office: office,
            Pin: Pin || null,
          },
          original_data: {
            Address: oldAddr,
            Office: oldOff,
            Pin: oldPin || null,
          },
          requested_by: session.user.id,
        };

        if (isCurrentUserAdmin) adminDirectEdits.push(reqData);
        else requests.push(reqData);
      } else {
        const reqData = {
          action_type: "ADD",
          proposed_data: {
            Address: address,
            Office: office,
            Pin: Pin || null,
          },
          requested_by: session.user.id,
        };

        if (isCurrentUserAdmin) adminDirectEdits.push(reqData);
        else requests.push(reqData);
      }
    }
  });

  if (requests.length === 0 && adminDirectEdits.length === 0) {
    return showToast(
      skippedParentOffice > 0
        ? "â„¹ï¸ Parent HO/SO staff is managed from the Directory office header (ðŸ‘¥ Staff)."
        : "Please fill in at least one complete row.",
    );
  }

  const btn = document.getElementById("submit-multiple-btn");
  setButtonState(btn, "loading", "Submitting...");

  try {
    if (isCurrentUserAdmin) {
      const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc("admin_direct_submit", {
        request_payloads: adminDirectEdits,
      });
      if (error) throw error;
      showToast("âœ… Admin: Changes applied instantly!");
      await fetchAllPostOffices(true);
    } else {
      const { error } = await supabaseClient
        .from("edit_requests")
        .insert(requests);
      if (error) throw error;
      showToast("âœ… Change requests submitted for approval!");
    }

    invalidateApprovalsCache();
    document.getElementById("manual-entries-container").innerHTML =
      getManualEntryRowHTML(true);
    switchDashboardView("search");
  } catch (err) {
    alert("Error submitting request: " + err.message);
  }

  setButtonState(btn, "idle");
}

// --- BULK SAME OFFICE UPLOAD TAB ---
let bulkReturnToDirectory = false;

function switchManualUploadTab(tab) {
  const rowsTab = document.getElementById("tab-manual-rows");
  const bulkTab = document.getElementById("tab-manual-bulk");
  const rowsPanel = document.getElementById("manual-panel-rows");
  const bulkPanel = document.getElementById("manual-panel-bulk");

  if (tab === "bulk") {
    rowsTab?.classList.remove("active");
    bulkTab?.classList.add("active");
    rowsPanel?.classList.add("hidden");
    bulkPanel?.classList.remove("hidden");
  } else {
    bulkTab?.classList.remove("active");
    rowsTab?.classList.add("active");
    bulkPanel?.classList.add("hidden");
    rowsPanel?.classList.remove("hidden");
  }
}

function selectBulkOffice(officeName, pinNumber) {
  const offInput = document.getElementById("bulk-office-input");
  const pinInput = document.getElementById("bulk-pin-input");
  const clearBtn = document.getElementById("bulk-office-clear");

  offInput.value = officeName;
  if (pinInput) pinInput.value = pinNumber;
  clearBtn?.classList.remove("hidden");

  closeAllDropdowns();
  updateBulkAddressPreview();
  document.getElementById("bulk-addresses-input")?.focus();
}

function clearBulkOffice() {
  const offInput = document.getElementById("bulk-office-input");
  const pinInput = document.getElementById("bulk-pin-input");
  if (offInput) offInput.value = "";
  if (pinInput) pinInput.value = "";
  offInput?.focus();
  handleBulkOfficeInput(offInput);
}

function parseBulkAddresses() {
  const raw = document.getElementById("bulk-addresses-input")?.value || "";
  const seen = new Set();
  const list = [];

  raw
    .split(/[\n,]+| {2,}/)
    .map((s) => s.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .forEach((addr) => {
      const key = addr.toLowerCase();
      if (!seen.has(key)) {
        seen.add(key);
        list.push(addr);
      }
    });

  return list;
}

function updateBulkAddressPreview() {
  const addresses = parseBulkAddresses();
  const box = document.getElementById("bulk-preview-box");
  const countEl = document.getElementById("bulk-preview-count");
  const chipsEl = document.getElementById("bulk-preview-chips");
  const submitBtn = document.getElementById("submit-bulk-btn");
  const officeVal = (document.getElementById("bulk-office-input")?.value || "")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();

  if (addresses.length === 0) {
    box?.classList.add("hidden");
    if (submitBtn) submitBtn.textContent = "Submit All Addresses";
    return;
  }

  const existingExact = new Map();
  const existingPhonetic = new Map();

  if (officeVal && cachedPostOffices) {
    cachedPostOffices.forEach((r) => {
      if (
        String(r.Office || "")
          .trim()
          .toLowerCase() === officeVal
      ) {
        const rawAddr = String(r.Address || "").trim();
        existingExact.set(rawAddr.toLowerCase(), rawAddr);
        const pKey = postalPhoneticKey(rawAddr);
        if (pKey && !existingPhonetic.has(pKey)) {
          existingPhonetic.set(pKey, rawAddr);
        }
      }
    });
  }

  let dupCount = 0;
  chipsEl.innerHTML = addresses
    .map((addr, idx) => {
      const matchedName =
        existingExact.get(addr.toLowerCase()) ||
        existingPhonetic.get(postalPhoneticKey(addr)) ||
        null;
      const isDup = Boolean(matchedName);
      if (isDup) dupCount++;
      return `
        <span class="bulk-chip ${isDup ? "is-duplicate" : ""}" title="${
          isDup ? `Similar to existing: ${escapeHTML(matchedName)}` : ""
        }">
          ${isDup ? "âš ï¸ " : ""}${escapeHTML(addr)}
          <button type="button" onclick="removeBulkAddressAt(${idx})" title="Remove">âœ•</button>
        </span>`;
    })
    .join("");

  countEl.textContent = `${addresses.length} ${
    addresses.length === 1 ? "address" : "addresses"
  } ready${dupCount > 0 ? ` (${dupCount} already in DB)` : ""}`;

  if (submitBtn) {
    submitBtn.textContent = `Submit ${addresses.length} ${
      addresses.length === 1 ? "Address" : "Addresses"
    }`;
  }
  box?.classList.remove("hidden");
}

function removeBulkAddressAt(indexToRemove) {
  const addresses = parseBulkAddresses();
  addresses.splice(indexToRemove, 1);
  const textarea = document.getElementById("bulk-addresses-input");
  if (textarea) textarea.value = addresses.join("\n");
  updateBulkAddressPreview();
}

function clearBulkAddresses() {
  const textarea = document.getElementById("bulk-addresses-input");
  if (textarea) textarea.value = "";
  updateBulkAddressPreview();
}

async function submitBulkSameOffice() {
  if (!(await ensureOnline("Connect to internet to save changes."))) return;

  const office = document.getElementById("bulk-office-input")?.value.trim();
  const pin = document.getElementById("bulk-pin-input")?.value.trim();
  const rawAddresses = parseBulkAddresses();

  if (!office) return showToast("âš ï¸ Please select or enter an Office first.");
  if (rawAddresses.length === 0) {
    return showToast("âš ï¸ Please enter at least one address.");
  }

  const addresses = rawAddresses.filter(
    (addr) => !isSelfParentOfficeRow(addr, office),
  );
  if (addresses.length === 0) {
    return showToast(
      "â„¹ï¸ Parent office staff is managed from the Directory office header (ðŸ‘¥ Staff).",
    );
  }

  const {
    data: { session },
  } = await supabaseClient.auth.getSession();

  if (!session || !session.user) {
    checkUserSession();
    return alert("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  const payloads = addresses.map((addr) => ({
    action_type: "ADD",
    proposed_data: {
      Address: addr,
      Office: office,
      Pin: pin || null,
    },
    requested_by: session.user.id,
  }));

  const btn = document.getElementById("submit-bulk-btn");
  setButtonState(btn, "loading", `Submitting ${addresses.length}...`);

  try {
    if (isCurrentUserAdmin) {
      const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc("admin_direct_submit", {
        request_payloads: payloads,
      });
      if (error) throw error;
      showToast(`âœ… Admin: Added ${addresses.length} addresses to ${office}!`);
      await fetchAllPostOffices(true);
    } else {
      const { error } = await supabaseClient
        .from("edit_requests")
        .insert(payloads);
      if (error) throw error;
      showToast(`âœ… ${addresses.length} addresses submitted for approval!`);
    }

    invalidateApprovalsCache();
    clearBulkAddresses();

    if (bulkReturnToDirectory) {
      bulkReturnToDirectory = false;
      switchDashboardView("directory");
    } else {
      switchDashboardView("search");
    }
  } catch (err) {
    alert("Error submitting addresses: " + err.message);
  } finally {
    setButtonState(btn, "idle");
    updateBulkAddressPreview();
  }
}

// --- OFFICE AUTOCOMPLETE DROPDOWNS (INLINE EDIT, MANUAL, BULK & SEARCH FILTER) ---
function buildOfficeOptionHTML(office, pin, mode) {
  return `<div class="autocomplete-item" onmousedown="pickOfficeOption(this, '${escapeForOnclick(
    office,
  )}', '${escapeForOnclick(pin)}', '${mode}')">
    <div class="ac-item-main">
      <span class="ac-item-icon">
        <svg width="15" height="15" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
          <rect x="4" y="2" width="16" height="20" rx="2" ry="2"></rect>
          <path d="M9 22v-4h6v4"></path>
          <path d="M8 6h.01"></path><path d="M16 6h.01"></path>
          <path d="M12 6h.01"></path><path d="M12 10h.01"></path>
        </svg>
      </span>
      <span class="ac-item-name">${escapeHTML(office)}</span>
    </div>
    ${pin ? `<span class="ac-item-pin">${escapeHTML(pin)}</span>` : ""}
  </div>`;
}

function pickOfficeOption(itemEl, office, pin, mode) {
  if (mode === "inline") {
    const input = document.getElementById("inline-edit-office");
    const pinInput = document.getElementById("inline-edit-pin");
    const clearBtn = document.getElementById("inline-edit-office-clear");
    if (input) input.value = office;
    if (pinInput && pin) pinInput.value = pin;
    if (clearBtn) clearBtn.classList.remove("hidden");
  } else if (mode === "manual") {
    const wrapper = itemEl.closest(".autocomplete-wrapper");
    const row = wrapper?.closest(".manual-entry-row");
    const input = wrapper?.querySelector(".manual-office");
    const clearBtn = wrapper?.querySelector(".clear-btn");
    const pinInput = row?.querySelector(".manual-Pin");
    if (input) input.value = office;
    if (pinInput && pin) pinInput.value = pin;
    if (clearBtn) clearBtn.classList.remove("hidden");
  } else if (mode === "bulk") {
    const input = document.getElementById("bulk-office-input");
    const pinInput = document.getElementById("bulk-pin-input");
    const clearBtn = document.getElementById("bulk-office-clear");
    if (input) input.value = office;
    if (pinInput && pin) pinInput.value = pin;
    if (clearBtn) clearBtn.classList.remove("hidden");
    updateBulkAddressPreview();
  } else if (mode === "search-filter") {
    selectSearchOfficeFilter(office);
    return;
  } else if (mode === "dir-filter") {
    selectDirectoryOfficeFilter(office);
    return;
  }
  closeAllDropdowns();
}

function getOfficePinMap() {
  const officeMap = new Map();
  if (cachedPostOffices) {
    cachedPostOffices.forEach((row) => {
      if (row.Office && !officeMap.has(row.Office)) {
        officeMap.set(row.Office, row.Pin || "");
      }
    });
  }
  return officeMap;
}

function handleInlineEditOfficeInput(inputElement) {
  cancelCloseDropdowns();
  const val = inputElement.value;
  const dropdown = document.getElementById("inline-edit-office-dropdown");
  const clearBtn = document.getElementById("inline-edit-office-clear");
  const pinInput = document.getElementById("inline-edit-pin");

  if (val.length > 0) clearBtn.classList.remove("hidden");
  else clearBtn.classList.add("hidden");

  const officeMap = getOfficePinMap();
  let matchedOffices = Array.from(officeMap.keys());
  if (val) {
    const lowerVal = val.toLowerCase();
    matchedOffices = matchedOffices.filter((o) =>
      o.toLowerCase().includes(lowerVal),
    );
  }
  matchedOffices.sort();

  if (matchedOffices.length === 0) {
    dropdown.classList.remove("show");
  } else {
    dropdown.innerHTML = matchedOffices
      .slice(0, 10)
      .map((office) =>
        buildOfficeOptionHTML(office, officeMap.get(office), "inline"),
      )
      .join("");
    dropdown.classList.add("show");
  }

  if (pinInput) {
    if (val.trim() === "") {
      pinInput.value = "";
    } else {
      const exactMatch = Array.from(officeMap.keys()).find(
        (k) => k.toLowerCase() === val.toLowerCase(),
      );
      if (exactMatch) pinInput.value = officeMap.get(exactMatch);
    }
  }
}

function clearInlineEditOffice() {
  const offInput = document.getElementById("inline-edit-office");
  offInput.value = "";
  offInput.focus();
  handleInlineEditOfficeInput(offInput);
}

function handleOfficeInput(inputElement) {
  cancelCloseDropdowns();
  const val = inputElement.value;
  const wrapper = inputElement.closest(".autocomplete-wrapper");
  const dropdown = wrapper.querySelector(".autocomplete-dropdown");
  const clearBtn = wrapper.querySelector(".clear-btn");
  const pinInput = wrapper
    .closest(".manual-entry-row")
    .querySelector(".manual-Pin");

  if (val.length > 0) clearBtn.classList.remove("hidden");
  else clearBtn.classList.add("hidden");

  const officeMap = getOfficePinMap();
  let matchedOffices = Array.from(officeMap.keys());
  if (val) {
    const lowerVal = val.toLowerCase();
    matchedOffices = matchedOffices.filter((o) =>
      o.toLowerCase().includes(lowerVal),
    );
  }
  matchedOffices.sort();

  if (matchedOffices.length === 0) {
    dropdown.classList.remove("show");
  } else {
    dropdown.innerHTML = matchedOffices
      .slice(0, 10)
      .map((office) =>
        buildOfficeOptionHTML(office, officeMap.get(office), "manual"),
      )
      .join("");
    dropdown.classList.add("show");
  }

  if (pinInput) {
    if (val.trim() === "") {
      pinInput.value = "";
    } else {
      const exactMatch = Array.from(officeMap.keys()).find(
        (k) => k.toLowerCase() === val.toLowerCase(),
      );
      if (exactMatch) pinInput.value = officeMap.get(exactMatch);
    }
  }
}

function clearOfficeInput(btnElement) {
  const wrapper = btnElement.closest(".autocomplete-wrapper");
  const input = wrapper.querySelector(".manual-office");

  input.value = "";
  input.focus();
  handleOfficeInput(input);
}

function handleBulkOfficeInput(inputElement) {

  const pinInput = document.getElementById("bulk-pin-input");

  const officeMap = getOfficePinMap();

  if (val) {
    const lowerVal = val.toLowerCase();
    matchedOffices = matchedOffices.filter((o) =>
      o.toLowerCase().includes(lowerVal),
    );
  }
  matchedOffices.sort();

  if (matchedOffices.length === 0) {
    dropdown.classList.remove("show");
  } else {
    dropdown.innerHTML = matchedOffices
      .slice(0, 10)
      .map((office) =>
        buildOfficeOptionHTML(office, officeMap.get(office), "bulk"),
      )
      .join("");
    dropdown.classList.add("show");
  }

  if (pinInput) {
    if (val.trim() === "") {
      pinInput.value = "";
    } else {

        (k) => k.toLowerCase() === val.toLowerCase(),
      );
      if (exactMatch) pinInput.value = officeMap.get(exactMatch);
    }
  }

  updateBulkAddressPreview();
}

      o.toLowerCase().includes(lowerVal),

      .slice(0, 10)

        buildOfficeOptionHTML(office, officeMap.get(office), "search-filter"),

      .join("");

  }
}

// --- EXCEL BULK UPLOAD & TEMPLATE ---
async function uploadExcel() {
  if (!(await ensureOnline("Connect to internet to save changes."))) return;
  await ensureXLSXLoaded();
  const fileInput = document.getElementById("excel-file");
  const file = fileInput.files[0];
  const uploadBtn = document.getElementById("upload-excel-btn");
  if (!file) return alert("Please select an Excel file first.");

  setButtonState(uploadBtn, "loading", "Uploading...");

  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = async function (e) {
    try {
      const data = new Uint8Array(e.target.result);
      const workbook = XLSX.read(data, { type: "array" });
      const jsonData = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(
        workbook.Sheets[workbook.SheetNames[0]],
      );
      if (jsonData.length === 0) {
        alert("The Excel file is empty.");
        setButtonState(uploadBtn, "idle");
        return;
      }

      const {
        data: { session },
      } = await supabaseClient.auth.getSession();

      if (!session || !session.user) {
        setButtonState(uploadBtn, "idle");
        checkUserSession();
        return alert("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
      }

      const formattedData = jsonData
        .filter((row) => !isSelfParentOfficeRow(row["Address"], row["Office"]))
        .map((row) => {
          const rawPrio = String(row["Priority"] ?? "")
            .trim()
            .toLowerCase();
          const priorityVal =
            rawPrio === "1" ||
            rawPrio === "high" ||
            rawPrio === "frequent" ||
            rawPrio === "yes" ||
            rawPrio === "true"
              ? 1
              : 0;

          return {
            Address: row["Address"],
            Office: row["Office"],
            Pin: row["Pin"] ? parseInt(row["Pin"], 10) || null : null,
            priority: priorityVal,
            user_id: session.user.id,
            last_modified_by: session.user.id,
            last_approved_by: session.user.id,
            upload_method: "excel_bulk",
            is_deleted: false,
          };
        });

      if (formattedData.length === 0) {
        showToast("â„¹ï¸ No routable address rows found to upload.");
        setButtonState(uploadBtn, "idle");
        return;
      }

      const { error } = await supabaseClient
        .from("post_offices")
        .insert(formattedData);
      if (error) {
        alert("Upload Error: " + error.message);
        setButtonState(uploadBtn, "idle");
      } else {
        setButtonState(uploadBtn, "success", "Uploaded!");
        showToast("âœ… Excel data uploaded successfully!");
        fileInput.value = "";
        await fetchAllPostOffices(true);
        setTimeout(() => {
          setButtonState(uploadBtn, "idle");
        }, 2000);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      alert("Error reading Excel file.");
      setButtonState(uploadBtn, "idle");
    }
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
}

async function downloadSampleExcel() {
  await ensureXLSXLoaded();
  const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
  const sampleData = [
    { Address: "Gothani BO", Office: "Chopan SO", Pin: "231205" },
    { Address: "Biraura BO", Office: "Bijaipur SO", Pin: "231305" },
  ];
  const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(sampleData);
  ws["!cols"] = [{ wch: 20 }, { wch: 20 }, { wch: 15 }];
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "DakMitra Template");
  XLSX.writeFile(wb, "DakMitra_Sample_Template.xlsx");
  showToast("ðŸ“¥ Sample Excel downloaded!");
}

// --- SAVED FAVORITES ---
function toggleFavorite(id, address, office, pin, btnElement) {
  let favorites = getSafeStorage(FAV_KEY);
  const existingIndex = favorites.findIndex((f) => String(f.id) === String(id));
  const isFavNow = existingIndex === -1;

  if (existingIndex > -1) {
    favorites.splice(existingIndex, 1);
    showToast("Removed from favorites");
  } else {
    favorites.push({ id, address, office, pin });
    showToast("â­ Added to favorites");
  }

  localStorage.setItem(FAV_KEY, JSON.stringify(favorites));
  renderFavorites();

  if (btnElement) {
    if (isFavNow) {
      btnElement.innerHTML = `${ICON_STAR_FILLED} Saved`;
      btnElement.style.color = "#f59e0b";
      btnElement.style.backgroundColor = "rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1)";
    } else {
      btnElement.innerHTML = `${ICON_STAR_OUTLINE} Save`;
      btnElement.style.color = "var(--text-light)";
      btnElement.style.backgroundColor = "var(--input-bg)";
    }
  } else if (typeof renderResults === "function") {
    renderResults();
  }
}

function renderFavorites() {
  const favorites = getSafeStorage(FAV_KEY);
  const displayArea = document.getElementById("favorites-display-area");
  if (!displayArea) return;

  if (favorites.length === 0) {
    displayArea.innerHTML = `
        <ul class="list-container">
          <li class="list-empty">
            <svg class="empty-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
              <path d="M20.84 4.61a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.78 0L12 5.67l-1.06-1.06a5.5 5.5 0 0 0-7.78 7.78l1.06 1.06L12 21.23l7.78-7.78 1.06-1.06a5.5 5.5 0 0 0 0-7.78z"></path>
            </svg>
            <span>No favorites saved yet.</span>
          </li>
        </ul>`;
    return;
  }

  let html = `<ul class="list-container" style="margin:0; list-style:none;">`;
  favorites.forEach((row) => {
    const favStyle = getFavoriteBtnStyles(true);

    html += `
        <li class="list-item" style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; gap: 12px;">
          <div style="flex: 1 1 200px;">
            <div class="office-title">${escapeHTML(row.address)}</div>
            <div class="sub-office">
              <span class="badge badge-primary">ðŸ¢ ${escapeHTML(
                row.office,
              )}</span>
              <span class="badge">ðŸ“ ${escapeHTML(row.pin) || "N/A"}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-end;">
            <button onclick="toggleFavorite('${
              row.id
            }', '${escapeForOnclick(row.address)}', '${escapeForOnclick(
              row.office,
            )}', '${escapeForOnclick(
              row.pin,
            )}', null)" class="toolbar-btn" style="padding: 6px 12px; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; border: 1px solid transparent; border-radius: 8px; cursor: pointer; transition: 0.2s; ${
              favStyle.style
            }">
              ${favStyle.icon}
            </button>
          </div>
        </li>`;
  });
  html += `</ul>`;
  displayArea.innerHTML = html;
}

// --- APPROVALS WORKFLOW (FULL SEARCH, PHONETIC & FILTER SUITE) ---
const APPROVALS_ACTION_TYPES = ["ADD", "EDIT", "DELETE"];

const approvalsFilters = {
  search: "",
  actionTypes: new Set(), // empty = ALL; subset of ADD / EDIT / DELETE
  userId: null,
  userName: "",
  userEmpId: "",
  userRole: "requested", // 'requested' | 'reviewed' | 'voted' | 'any'
  datePreset: "any", // 'any' | 'today' | 'yesterday' | '7d' | 'month' | 'specific' | 'range'
  dateSingle: "",
  dateFrom: "",
  dateTo: "",
  office: "",
  pin: "",
  pendingScope: "all", // 'all' | 'needs_my_vote' | 'my_requests' | 'i_voted'
  voteProgress: "all", // 'all' | 'zero' | 'near_approval'
  resolvedStatus: "all", // 'all' | 'APPROVED' | 'REJECTED'
  approvalSource: "all", // 'all' | 'admin_approved' | 'admin_direct' | 'community'
  resolvedOnlyMine: false,
  sort: "newest", // 'newest' | 'oldest' | 'most_votes'
};

let approvalsPage = 0;
let approvalsHasMore = true;
let approvalsTotalCount = 0;
let approvalsRequestSeq = 0;
let approvalsSearchDebounce = null;
let approvalsPinDebounce = null;
let approvalsOfficeDebounce = null;
let approvalsRealtimeTimer = null;
let approvalsUsersLoadingPromise = null;
let approvalsSkipNextViewSwitchLoad = false;

function hasActiveApprovalFilters(tab = currentApprovalTab) {
  if (approvalsFilters.search.trim().length > 0) return true;
  if (approvalsFilters.actionTypes.size > 0) return true;
  if (approvalsFilters.userId) return true;
  if (approvalsFilters.datePreset !== "any") return true;
  if (approvalsFilters.office.trim().length > 0) return true;
  if (approvalsFilters.pin.trim().length > 0) return true;
  if (approvalsFilters.sort !== "newest") return true;

  if (tab === "pending") {
    if (approvalsFilters.pendingScope !== "all") return true;
    if (approvalsFilters.voteProgress !== "all") return true;
  } else {
    if (approvalsFilters.resolvedStatus !== "all") return true;
    if (approvalsFilters.approvalSource !== "all") return true;
    if (approvalsFilters.resolvedOnlyMine) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

function getAdvancedApprovalFilterCount(tab = currentApprovalTab) {
  let count = 0;
  if (approvalsFilters.userId) count++;
  if (approvalsFilters.datePreset !== "any") count++;
  if (approvalsFilters.office.trim().length > 0) count++;
  if (approvalsFilters.pin.trim().length > 0) count++;
  if (approvalsFilters.sort !== "newest") count++;
  if (tab === "pending" && approvalsFilters.voteProgress !== "all") count++;
  if (tab === "resolved" && approvalsFilters.approvalSource !== "all") count++;
  return count;
}

function parseLocalDateISO(dateStr, addDays = 0) {
  if (!dateStr || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(dateStr)) return null;
  const [y, m, d] = dateStr.split("-").map(Number);
  return new Date(y, m - 1, d + addDays, 0, 0, 0, 0).toISOString();
}

function computeApprovalsDateBounds() {
  const preset = approvalsFilters.datePreset;
  if (preset === "any") return { from: null, to: null };

  const now = new Date();
  const y = now.getFullYear();
  const m = now.getMonth();
  const d = now.getDate();

  if (preset === "today") {
    return {
      from: new Date(y, m, d, 0, 0, 0, 0).toISOString(),
      to: new Date(y, m, d + 1, 0, 0, 0, 0).toISOString(),
    };
  }
  if (preset === "yesterday") {
    return {
      from: new Date(y, m, d - 1, 0, 0, 0, 0).toISOString(),
      to: new Date(y, m, d, 0, 0, 0, 0).toISOString(),
    };
  }
  if (preset === "7d") {
    return {
      from: new Date(y, m, d - 6, 0, 0, 0, 0).toISOString(),
      to: new Date(y, m, d + 1, 0, 0, 0, 0).toISOString(),
    };
  }
  if (preset === "month") {
    return {
      from: new Date(y, m, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0).toISOString(),
      to: new Date(y, m, d + 1, 0, 0, 0, 0).toISOString(),
    };
  }
  if (preset === "specific") {
    if (!approvalsFilters.dateSingle) return { from: null, to: null };
    return {
      from: parseLocalDateISO(approvalsFilters.dateSingle, 0),
      to: parseLocalDateISO(approvalsFilters.dateSingle, 1),
    };
  }
  if (preset === "range") {
    let fromStr = approvalsFilters.dateFrom;
    let toStr = approvalsFilters.dateTo;
    if (fromStr && toStr && fromStr > toStr) {
      [fromStr, toStr] = [toStr, fromStr];
    }
    return {
      from: parseLocalDateISO(fromStr, 0),
      to: parseLocalDateISO(toStr, 1),
    };
  }
  return { from: null, to: null };
}

function highlightApprovalText(text, query) {
  const str = text === null || text === undefined ? "" : String(text);
  const q = String(query || "").trim();
  if (!str || q.length < 2) return escapeHTML(str);

  const lowerStr = str.toLowerCase();
  const lowerQ = q.toLowerCase();
  let out = "";
  let pos = 0;
  let idx = lowerStr.indexOf(lowerQ, pos);

  if (idx === -1) return escapeHTML(str);

  while (idx !== -1) {
    out += escapeHTML(str.slice(pos, idx));
    out += `<span class="highlight-text">${escapeHTML(
      str.slice(idx, idx + q.length),
    )}</span>`;
    pos = idx + q.length;
    idx = lowerStr.indexOf(lowerQ, pos);
  }
  out += escapeHTML(str.slice(pos));
  return out;
}

// --- FILTER CONTROLS & UI SYNC ---
function syncApprovalsFilterUI() {
  const isPending = currentApprovalTab === "pending";

  // Keep sort valid on Resolved tab
  if (!isPending && approvalsFilters.sort === "most_votes") {
    approvalsFilters.sort = "newest";
  }

  // Keep user role valid on Pending tab
  if (isPending && approvalsFilters.userRole === "reviewed") {
    approvalsFilters.userRole = "requested";
  }

  // Toggle tab-specific sections inside Filters drawer
  document
    .getElementById("appr-vote-progress-section")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", !isPending);
  document
    .getElementById("appr-approval-source-section")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", isPending);
  document
    .getElementById("appr-sort-votes-btn")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", !isPending);
  document
    .getElementById("appr-role-reviewed-btn")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", isPending);

  // Toggle tab-specific pinned quick chips
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-needs-vote")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", !isPending);
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-my-pending")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", !isPending);
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-i-voted")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", !isPending);

  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-approved")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", isPending);
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-rejected")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", isPending);
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-my-resolved")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", isPending);

  // Sync Change Type chips
  const isAllActions = approvalsFilters.actionTypes.size === 0;
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-all")
    ?.classList.toggle("active", isAllActions);
  APPROVALS_ACTION_TYPES.forEach((act) => {
    document
      .getElementById(`appr-chip-${act}`)
      ?.classList.toggle("active", approvalsFilters.actionTypes.has(act));
  });

  // Sync Pending contextual chips
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-needs-vote")
    ?.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      approvalsFilters.pendingScope === "needs_my_vote",
    );
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-my-pending")
    ?.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      approvalsFilters.pendingScope === "my_requests",
    );
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-i-voted")
    ?.classList.toggle("active", approvalsFilters.pendingScope === "i_voted");

  // Sync Resolved contextual chips
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-approved")
    ?.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      approvalsFilters.resolvedStatus === "APPROVED",
    );
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-rejected")
    ?.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      approvalsFilters.resolvedStatus === "REJECTED",
    );
  document
    .getElementById("appr-chip-my-resolved")
    ?.classList.toggle("active", approvalsFilters.resolvedOnlyMine);

  // Sync User role row
  const roleRow = document.getElementById("appr-user-role-row");
  if (roleRow) {
    roleRow.classList.toggle("hidden", !approvalsFilters.userId);
    roleRow.querySelectorAll("[data-user-role]").forEach((btn) => {
      btn.classList.toggle(
        "active",
        btn.getAttribute("data-user-role") === approvalsFilters.userRole,
      );
    });
  }

  // Sync Date presets & pickers
  document.querySelectorAll("[data-date-preset]").forEach((btn) => {
    btn.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      btn.getAttribute("data-date-preset") === approvalsFilters.datePreset,
    );
  });
  document
    .getElementById("appr-specific-date-wrap")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", approvalsFilters.datePreset !== "specific");
  document
    .getElementById("appr-range-date-wrap")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", approvalsFilters.datePreset !== "range");

  // Sync Vote Progress, Approval Source & Sort pills
  document.querySelectorAll("[data-vote-progress]").forEach((btn) => {
    btn.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      btn.getAttribute("data-vote-progress") === approvalsFilters.voteProgress,
    );
  });
  document.querySelectorAll("[data-approval-source]").forEach((btn) => {
    btn.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      btn.getAttribute("data-approval-source") ===
        approvalsFilters.approvalSource,
    );
  });
  document.querySelectorAll("[data-appr-sort]").forEach((btn) => {
    btn.classList.toggle(
      "active",
      btn.getAttribute("data-appr-sort") === approvalsFilters.sort,
    );
  });

  // Sync Filters toggle button badge & Search wrapper highlight
  const advCount = getAdvancedApprovalFilterCount();
  const toggleBtn = document.getElementById("approvals-filter-toggle-btn");
  const searchWrap = document.getElementById("approvals-search-wrapper");

  if (toggleBtn) {
    if (advCount > 0) {
      toggleBtn.classList.add("has-active-filters");
      toggleBtn.innerHTML = `<span class="filter-pulse-dot"></span><span>âš™ï¸ Filters</span><span class="filter-count-badge">${advCount}</span>`;
    } else {
      toggleBtn.classList.remove("has-active-filters");
      toggleBtn.innerHTML = "âš™ï¸ Filters";
    }
  }
  if (searchWrap) {
    searchWrap.classList.toggle("has-active-filters", advCount > 0);
  }

  renderApprovalsActiveFilterPills();
}

function renderApprovalsActiveFilterPills() {
  const bar = document.getElementById("approvals-active-filters-bar");
  if (!bar) return;

  const pills = [];

  if (approvalsFilters.userId) {
    const roleLabels = {
      requested: "By",
      reviewed: "Reviewed by",
      voted: "Voted by",
      any: "User",
    };
    const prefix = roleLabels[approvalsFilters.userRole] || "User";
    pills.push({
      key: "user",
      label: `ðŸ‘¤ ${prefix}: ${approvalsFilters.userName}`,
    });
  }

  if (approvalsFilters.datePreset !== "any") {
    const dateLabels = {
      today: "Today",
      yesterday: "Yesterday",
      "7d": "Last 7 Days",
      month: "This Month",
      specific: approvalsFilters.dateSingle || "Specific Date",
      range:
        approvalsFilters.dateFrom || approvalsFilters.dateTo
          ? `${approvalsFilters.dateFrom || "..."} â†’ ${
              approvalsFilters.dateTo || "..."
            }`
          : "Date Range",
    };
    pills.push({
      key: "date",
      label: `ðŸ“… ${dateLabels[approvalsFilters.datePreset]}`,
    });
  }

  if (approvalsFilters.office.trim()) {
    pills.push({
      key: "office",
      label: `ðŸ¢ ${approvalsFilters.office.trim()}`,
    });
  }

  if (approvalsFilters.pin.trim()) {
    pills.push({
      key: "pin",
      label: `ðŸ“ ${approvalsFilters.pin.trim()}`,
    });
  }

  if (
    currentApprovalTab === "pending" &&
    approvalsFilters.voteProgress !== "all"
  ) {
    pills.push({
      key: "voteProgress",
      label:
        approvalsFilters.voteProgress === "near_approval"
          ? "ðŸ”¥ Near Approval (3â€“4)"
          : "ðŸ—³ï¸ 0 Votes",
    });
  }

  if (
    currentApprovalTab === "resolved" &&
    approvalsFilters.approvalSource !== "all"
  ) {
    const srcLabels = {
      admin_approved: "ðŸ›¡ï¸ Admin Approved",
      admin_direct: "âš¡ Admin Direct",
      community: "ðŸ‘¥ Community",
    };
    pills.push({
      key: "approvalSource",
      label: srcLabels[approvalsFilters.approvalSource],
    });
  }

  if (approvalsFilters.sort !== "newest") {
    pills.push({
      key: "sort",
      label:
        approvalsFilters.sort === "oldest"
          ? "â†•ï¸ Oldest First"
          : "â†•ï¸ Most Votes",
    });
  }

  if (pills.length === 0) {
    bar.classList.add("hidden");
    bar.innerHTML = "";
    return;
  }

  let html = pills
    .map(
      (p) => `
      <span class="active-filter-pill">
        <span>${escapeHTML(p.label)}</span>
        <button type="button" onclick="removeSingleApprovalFilter('${p.key}')" title="Remove filter">âœ•</button>
      </span>`,
    )
    .join("");

  if (pills.length > 1) {
    html += `<button type="button" class="clear-all-filters-pill" onclick="resetApprovalsFilters()">Clear all</button>`;
  }

  bar.innerHTML = html;
  bar.classList.remove("hidden");
}

function updateApprovalsSummaryStrip(totalCount) {
  const summaryEl = document.getElementById("approvals-filter-summary");
  const textEl = document.getElementById("approvals-filter-summary-text");
  if (!summaryEl || !textEl) return;

  if (!hasActiveApprovalFilters()) {
    summaryEl.classList.add("hidden");
    textEl.textContent = "";
    return;
  }

  const label = totalCount === 1 ? "request" : "requests";
  textEl.textContent = `Showing ${totalCount} matching ${label}`;
  summaryEl.classList.remove("hidden");
}

function toggleApprovalsFiltersPanel() {
  const panel = document.getElementById("approvals-filters-panel");
  if (!panel) return;
  const willOpen = panel.classList.contains("hidden");
  panel.classList.toggle("hidden");
  if (willOpen) {
    ensureApprovalsUsersLoaded();
  } else {
    closeAllDropdowns();
  }
}

function handleApprovalsSearchInput(inputEl) {
  const val = inputEl ? inputEl.value : "";
  approvalsFilters.search = val;
  document
    .getElementById("clear-approvals-search-btn")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", val.length === 0);

  clearTimeout(approvalsSearchDebounce);
  approvalsSearchDebounce = setTimeout(() => {
    applyApprovalsFilters(true);
  }, 280);
}

function clearApprovalsSearchInput() {
  const input = document.getElementById("approvals-search-input");
  if (input) {
    input.value = "";
    input.focus();
  }
  approvalsFilters.search = "";
  document
    .getElementById("clear-approvals-search-btn")
    ?.classList.add("hidden");
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function toggleApprovalsActionChip(action) {
  if (action === "ALL") {
    approvalsFilters.actionTypes.clear();
  } else if (APPROVALS_ACTION_TYPES.includes(action)) {
    if (approvalsFilters.actionTypes.has(action)) {
      approvalsFilters.actionTypes.delete(action);
    } else {
      approvalsFilters.actionTypes.add(action);
    }
    if (approvalsFilters.actionTypes.size === APPROVALS_ACTION_TYPES.length) {
      approvalsFilters.actionTypes.clear();
    }
  }
  if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(18);
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function setApprovalsPendingScope(scope) {
  approvalsFilters.pendingScope =
    approvalsFilters.pendingScope === scope ? "all" : scope;

  // Avoid conflicting user filter when toggling "My Requests"
  if (
    approvalsFilters.pendingScope === "my_requests" &&
    approvalsFilters.userId &&
    String(approvalsFilters.userId) !== String(currentUserId)
  ) {
    clearApprovalsUserFilter(false);
  }
  if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(18);
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function setApprovalsResolvedStatus(status) {
  approvalsFilters.resolvedStatus =
    approvalsFilters.resolvedStatus === status ? "all" : status;

  // Approval source only applies to APPROVED requests
  if (approvalsFilters.resolvedStatus === "REJECTED") {
    approvalsFilters.approvalSource = "all";
  }
  if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(18);
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function toggleApprovalsResolvedMine() {
  approvalsFilters.resolvedOnlyMine = !approvalsFilters.resolvedOnlyMine;
  if (
    approvalsFilters.resolvedOnlyMine &&
    approvalsFilters.userId &&
    String(approvalsFilters.userId) !== String(currentUserId)
  ) {
    clearApprovalsUserFilter(false);
  }
  if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(18);
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

// --- SPECIFIC USER AUTOCOMPLETE (REUSES MEMBERS DIRECTORY RPC) ---
async function ensureApprovalsUsersLoaded() {
  if (
    typeof membersDirectoryData !== "undefined" &&
    Array.isArray(membersDirectoryData) &&
    membersDirectoryData.length > 0
  ) {
    return membersDirectoryData;
  }

  try {
    const cached = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MEMBERS_CACHE_KEY));
    if (Array.isArray(cached) && cached.length > 0) {
      membersDirectoryData = cached;
      return membersDirectoryData;
    }
  } catch (_) {}

  if (!navigator.onLine) return [];

  if (!approvalsUsersLoadingPromise) {
    approvalsUsersLoadingPromise = (async () => {
      try {
        await ensureSupabaseReady();
        const { data, error } = await supabaseClient.rpc(
          "get_members_directory",
        );
        if (!error && Array.isArray(data)) {
          membersDirectoryData = data;
          try {
            localStorage.setItem(MEMBERS_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
          } catch (_) {}
        }
      } catch (_) {
      } finally {
        approvalsUsersLoadingPromise = null;
      }
      return membersDirectoryData || [];
    })();
  }
  return approvalsUsersLoadingPromise;
}

async function handleApprovalsUserInput(inputEl) {
  cancelCloseDropdowns();
  const val = (inputEl?.value || "").trim();
  const dropdown = document.getElementById("appr-filter-user-dropdown");
  const clearBtn = document.getElementById("clear-appr-user-btn");

  clearBtn?.classList.toggle(
    "hidden",
    val.length === 0 && !approvalsFilters.userId,
  );

  // If user edits text away from selected user, clear selected ID until they pick
  if (
    approvalsFilters.userId &&
    val.toLowerCase() !== approvalsFilters.userName.toLowerCase()
  ) {
    approvalsFilters.userId = null;
    approvalsFilters.userName = "";
    approvalsFilters.userEmpId = "";
    syncApprovalsFilterUI();
  }

  const members = await ensureApprovalsUsersLoaded();
  if (!dropdown) return;

  const q = val.toLowerCase();
  const matches = members
    .filter((m) => {
      if (!q) return true;
      const hay = `${m.name || ""} ${m.employee_id || ""} ${
        m.designation || ""
      }`.toLowerCase();
      return hay.includes(q);
    })
    .slice(0, 10);

  if (matches.length === 0) {
    dropdown.classList.remove("show");
    return;
  }

  dropdown.innerHTML = matches
    .map((m) => {
      const mName = m.name || "DakMitra User";
      const empId = m.employee_id || "No ID";
      const initial = mName.trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "U";
      return `
        <div
          class="autocomplete-item"
          onpointerdown="event.preventDefault()"
          onclick="selectApprovalsUser('${escapeForOnclick(
            m.id,
          )}', '${escapeForOnclick(mName)}', '${escapeForOnclick(empId)}')"
        >
          <div class="ac-item-main">
            <span class="ac-item-icon" style="font-weight: 800; font-size: 12px;">${escapeHTML(
              initial,
            )}</span>
            <span class="ac-item-name">${escapeHTML(mName)}${
              m.is_admin ? " ðŸ›¡ï¸" : ""
            }</span>
          </div>
          <span class="ac-item-pin">${escapeHTML(empId)}</span>
        </div>`;
    })
    .join("");
  dropdown.classList.add("show");
}

function selectApprovalsUser(userId, userName, userEmpId = "") {
  approvalsFilters.userId = String(userId);
  approvalsFilters.userName = userName || "User";
  approvalsFilters.userEmpId = userEmpId || "";

  // Clear conflicting "My Requests" toggle if another colleague was chosen
  if (String(userId) !== String(currentUserId)) {
    if (approvalsFilters.pendingScope === "my_requests") {
      approvalsFilters.pendingScope = "all";
    }
    approvalsFilters.resolvedOnlyMine = false;
  }

  const input = document.getElementById("appr-filter-user");
  if (input) input.value = approvalsFilters.userName;
  document.getElementById("clear-appr-user-btn")?.classList.remove("hidden");
  closeAllDropdowns();
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function clearApprovalsUserFilter(shouldRefresh = true) {
  approvalsFilters.userId = null;
  approvalsFilters.userName = "";
  approvalsFilters.userEmpId = "";
  approvalsFilters.userRole = "requested";

  const input = document.getElementById("appr-filter-user");
  if (input) input.value = "";
  document.getElementById("clear-appr-user-btn")?.classList.add("hidden");
  closeAllDropdowns();
  if (shouldRefresh) applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function setApprovalsUserRole(role) {
  approvalsFilters.userRole = role || "requested";
  if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(15);
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

// --- DATE, OFFICE, PIN, VOTE, SOURCE & SORT HANDLERS ---
function setApprovalsDatePreset(preset) {
  approvalsFilters.datePreset = preset || "any";

  const todayISO = (() => {
    const now = new Date();
    const y = now.getFullYear();
    const m = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
    const d = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
  })();

  if (preset === "specific") {
    if (!approvalsFilters.dateSingle) {
      approvalsFilters.dateSingle = todayISO;
      const el = document.getElementById("appr-date-single");
      if (el) el.value = todayISO;
    }
  } else if (preset === "range") {
    if (!approvalsFilters.dateFrom && !approvalsFilters.dateTo) {
      approvalsFilters.dateTo = todayISO;
      const toEl = document.getElementById("appr-date-to");
      if (toEl) toEl.value = todayISO;
    }
  } else if (preset === "any") {
    approvalsFilters.dateSingle = "";
    approvalsFilters.dateFrom = "";
    approvalsFilters.dateTo = "";
    const s = document.getElementById("appr-date-single");
    const f = document.getElementById("appr-date-from");
    const t = document.getElementById("appr-date-to");
    if (s) s.value = "";
    if (f) f.value = "";
    if (t) t.value = "";
  }

  if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(15);
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function handleApprovalsDateInputChange() {
  approvalsFilters.dateSingle =
    document.getElementById("appr-date-single")?.value || "";
  approvalsFilters.dateFrom =
    document.getElementById("appr-date-from")?.value || "";
  approvalsFilters.dateTo =
    document.getElementById("appr-date-to")?.value || "";
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function handleApprovalsOfficeInput(inputEl) {
  cancelCloseDropdowns();
  const val = inputEl?.value || "";
  approvalsFilters.office = val;

  document
    .getElementById("clear-appr-office-btn")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", val.length === 0);

  const dropdown = document.getElementById("appr-filter-office-dropdown");
  if (dropdown && typeof getOfficePinMap === "function") {
    const officeMap = getOfficePinMap();
    let matched = Array.from(officeMap.keys());
    if (val.trim()) {
      const lower = val.trim().toLowerCase();
      matched = matched.filter((o) => o.toLowerCase().includes(lower));
    }
    matched.sort();

    if (matched.length === 0) {
      dropdown.classList.remove("show");
    } else {
      dropdown.innerHTML = matched
        .slice(0, 10)
        .map((office) => {
          const pin = officeMap.get(office);
          return `
            <div
              class="autocomplete-item"
              onpointerdown="event.preventDefault()"
              onclick="selectApprovalsOffice('${escapeForOnclick(office)}')"
            >
              <div class="ac-item-main">
                <span class="ac-item-icon">ðŸ¢</span>
                <span class="ac-item-name">${escapeHTML(office)}</span>
              </div>
              ${
                pin ? `<span class="ac-item-pin">${escapeHTML(pin)}</span>` : ""
              }
            </div>`;
        })
        .join("");
      dropdown.classList.add("show");
    }
  }

  clearTimeout(approvalsOfficeDebounce);
  approvalsOfficeDebounce = setTimeout(() => {
    applyApprovalsFilters(true);
  }, 280);
}

function selectApprovalsOffice(officeName) {
  approvalsFilters.office = officeName || "";
  const input = document.getElementById("appr-filter-office");
  if (input) input.value = approvalsFilters.office;
  document.getElementById("clear-appr-office-btn")?.classList.remove("hidden");
  closeAllDropdowns();
  clearTimeout(approvalsOfficeDebounce);
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function clearApprovalsOfficeFilter() {
  approvalsFilters.office = "";
  const input = document.getElementById("appr-filter-office");
  if (input) input.value = "";
  document.getElementById("clear-appr-office-btn")?.classList.add("hidden");
  closeAllDropdowns();
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}
