  document
    .getElementById("clear-appr-pin-btn")
    ?.classList.toggle("hidden", val.length === 0);

  clearTimeout(approvalsPinDebounce);
  approvalsPinDebounce = setTimeout(() => {
    applyApprovalsFilters(true);
  }, 280);
}

function clearApprovalsPinFilter() {
  approvalsFilters.pin = "";
  const input = document.getElementById("appr-filter-pin");
  if (input) input.value = "";
  document.getElementById("clear-appr-pin-btn")?.classList.add("hidden");
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function setApprovalsVoteProgress(val) {
  approvalsFilters.voteProgress = val || "all";
  if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(15);
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function setApprovalsApprovalSource(val) {
  approvalsFilters.approvalSource = val || "all";
  if (approvalsFilters.approvalSource !== "all") {
    approvalsFilters.resolvedStatus = "APPROVED";
  }
  if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(15);
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function setApprovalsSort(val) {
  approvalsFilters.sort = val || "newest";
  if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(15);
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function removeSingleApprovalFilter(key) {
  if (key === "user") {
    clearApprovalsUserFilter(false);
  } else if (key === "date") {
    setApprovalsDatePreset("any");
    return;
  } else if (key === "office") {
    clearApprovalsOfficeFilter();
    return;
  } else if (key === "pin") {
    clearApprovalsPinFilter();
    return;
  } else if (key === "voteProgress") {
    approvalsFilters.voteProgress = "all";
  } else if (key === "approvalSource") {
    approvalsFilters.approvalSource = "all";
  } else if (key === "sort") {
    approvalsFilters.sort = "newest";
  }
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
}

function resetApprovalsFiltersStateOnly() {
  approvalsFilters.search = "";
  approvalsFilters.actionTypes.clear();
  approvalsFilters.userId = null;
  approvalsFilters.userName = "";
  approvalsFilters.userEmpId = "";
  approvalsFilters.userRole = "requested";
  approvalsFilters.datePreset = "any";
  approvalsFilters.dateSingle = "";
  approvalsFilters.dateFrom = "";
  approvalsFilters.dateTo = "";
  approvalsFilters.office = "";
  approvalsFilters.pin = "";
  approvalsFilters.pendingScope = "all";
  approvalsFilters.voteProgress = "all";
  approvalsFilters.resolvedStatus = "all";
  approvalsFilters.approvalSource = "all";
  approvalsFilters.resolvedOnlyMine = false;
  approvalsFilters.sort = "newest";

  const clearIds = [
    "approvals-search-input",
    "appr-filter-user",
    "appr-date-single",
    "appr-date-from",
    "appr-date-to",
    "appr-filter-office",
    "appr-filter-pin",
  ];
  clearIds.forEach((id) => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.value = "";
  });

  [
    "clear-approvals-search-btn",
    "clear-appr-user-btn",
    "clear-appr-office-btn",
    "clear-appr-pin-btn",
  ].forEach((id) => document.getElementById(id)?.classList.add("hidden"));

  closeAllDropdowns();
}

function resetApprovalsFilters() {
  resetApprovalsFiltersStateOnly();
  document.getElementById("approvals-filters-panel")?.classList.add("hidden");
  applyApprovalsFilters(true);
  showToast("ðŸ§¹ Approval filters cleared");
}

function applyApprovalsFilters(resetScroll = true) {
  syncApprovalsFilterUI();
  approvalsPage = 0;
  approvalsHasMore = true;
  approvalsTotalCount = 0;

  const displayArea = document.getElementById("approvals-display-area");
  if (resetScroll && displayArea) displayArea.scrollTop = 0;

  const isFiltered = hasActiveApprovalFilters(currentApprovalTab);
  const cache = approvalsCache[currentApprovalTab];

  // Use instant in-memory cache when unfiltered and already loaded
  if (!isFiltered && cache.loaded && cache.html) {
    approvalsRequestSeq++;
    isApprovalsLoading = false;
    const contentDiv = document.getElementById("approvals-content");
    const sentinel = document.getElementById("approvals-sentinel");
    approvalsPage = 1;
    approvalsHasMore = cache.initialHasMore;
    approvalsTotalCount = cache.totalCount || 0;
    if (contentDiv) contentDiv.innerHTML = cache.html;
    if (sentinel) {
      sentinel.style.display = cache.html.includes("list-empty")
        ? "none"
        : "flex";
      sentinel.innerHTML = !approvalsHasMore
        ? `<span style="color: var(--text-light); font-size: 13px;">End of requests</span>`
        : "";
    }
    updateApprovalsSummaryStrip(approvalsTotalCount);
    setupApprovalsObserver();
    return;
  }

  const contentDiv = document.getElementById("approvals-content");
  const sentinel = document.getElementById("approvals-sentinel");
  if (contentDiv) contentDiv.innerHTML = getApprovalsSkeletonHTML(3);
  if (sentinel) {
    sentinel.style.display = "flex";
    sentinel.innerHTML = "";
  }

  setupApprovalsObserver();
  loadApprovalsBatch(true);
}

// --- DIRECT JUMP SHORTCUT FROM USER PROFILE MODAL ---
function openApprovalsForUser(
  userId,
  userName = "",
  userEmpId = "",
  targetTab = "resolved",
  userRole = "requested",
) {
  if (!userId) return;

  if (typeof closeUserProfileModal === "function") closeUserProfileModal();
  if (typeof closeHistoryModal === "function") closeHistoryModal();

  resetApprovalsFiltersStateOnly();
  approvalsFilters.userId = String(userId);
  approvalsFilters.userName = userName || "User";
  approvalsFilters.userEmpId = userEmpId || "";
  approvalsFilters.userRole = userRole || "requested";

  const userInput = document.getElementById("appr-filter-user");
  if (userInput) userInput.value = approvalsFilters.userName;
  document.getElementById("clear-appr-user-btn")?.classList.remove("hidden");
  document.getElementById("approvals-filters-panel")?.classList.add("hidden");

  approvalsSkipNextViewSwitchLoad = true;
  if (typeof switchDashboardView === "function") {
    switchDashboardView("approvals");
  }
  approvalsSkipNextViewSwitchLoad = false;

  switchApprovalTab(targetTab === "pending" ? "pending" : "resolved", true);

  const roleText =
    userRole === "voted"
      ? "voted on by"
      : userRole === "reviewed"
        ? "reviewed by"
        : "by";
  showToast(`ðŸ“‹ Showing approvals ${roleText} ${approvalsFilters.userName}`);
}

// --- DEBOUNCED REALTIME REFRESH (PRESERVES ACTIVE FILTERS) ---
function scheduleApprovalsRealtimeRefresh() {
  invalidateApprovalsCache();
  clearTimeout(approvalsRealtimeTimer);
  approvalsRealtimeTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    if (
      document.getElementById("view-approvals")?.classList.contains("active")
    ) {
      switchApprovalTab(currentApprovalTab, true);
    }
  }, 260);
}

// --- SKELETON & TAB SWITCHING ---
function getApprovalsSkeletonHTML(count = 3) {
  let html = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    html += `
        <div class="approval-skeleton-card">
          <div class="skeleton-line skeleton-pill"></div>
          <div class="skeleton-line medium" style="height: 15px; margin-bottom: 8px;"></div>
          <div class="skeleton-line short" style="height: 14px; margin-bottom: 8px;"></div>
          <div class="skeleton-line" style="width: 28%; height: 14px; margin-bottom: 0;"></div>
          <div class="skeleton-meta-box">
            <div class="skeleton-line medium" style="height: 12px; margin-bottom: 8px;"></div>
            <div class="skeleton-line short" style="height: 12px; margin-bottom: 0;"></div>
          </div>
          ${
            currentApprovalTab === "pending"
              ? `<div class="skeleton-action-row">
                   <div class="skeleton-line skeleton-btn"></div>
                 </div>`
              : `<div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; margin-top: 12px;">
                   <div class="skeleton-line" style="width: 170px; height: 14px; margin-bottom: 0;"></div>
                 </div>`
          }
        </div>
      `;
  }
  return html;
}

function switchApprovalTab(tab, forceLoad = false) {
  if (approvalsSkipNextViewSwitchLoad && !forceLoad) return;

  const isSameTab = currentApprovalTab === tab;
  const isFiltered = hasActiveApprovalFilters(tab);
  const cache = approvalsCache[tab];
  const contentDiv = document.getElementById("approvals-content");

  if (
    isSameTab &&
    !forceLoad &&
    contentDiv &&
    contentDiv.innerHTML.trim() !== "" &&
    ((!isFiltered && cache.loaded) || isFiltered)
  ) {
    syncApprovalsFilterUI();
    return;
  }

  currentApprovalTab = tab;
  isApprovalsLoading = false;

  if (tab === "pending") {
    document.getElementById("tab-appr-pending")?.classList.add("active");
    document.getElementById("tab-appr-resolved")?.classList.remove("active");
  } else {
    document.getElementById("tab-appr-resolved")?.classList.add("active");
    document.getElementById("tab-appr-pending")?.classList.remove("active");
  }

  syncApprovalsFilterUI();

  const displayArea = document.getElementById("approvals-display-area");
  if (displayArea) displayArea.scrollTop = 0;

  if (!contentDiv && displayArea) {
    displayArea.innerHTML = `
      <div id="approvals-content" style="padding: 4px;"></div>
      <div id="approvals-sentinel" style="min-height: 54px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 10px;"></div>
    `;
  }

  const activeContent = document.getElementById("approvals-content");
  const sentinel = document.getElementById("approvals-sentinel");

  if (!isFiltered && cache.loaded && !forceLoad) {
    approvalsRequestSeq++;
    isApprovalsLoading = false;
    approvalsPage = 1;
    approvalsHasMore = cache.initialHasMore;
    approvalsTotalCount = cache.totalCount || 0;
    if (activeContent) activeContent.innerHTML = cache.html;
    if (sentinel) {
      sentinel.style.display = cache.html.includes("list-empty")
        ? "none"
        : "flex";
      sentinel.innerHTML = !approvalsHasMore
        ? `<span style="color: var(--text-light); font-size: 13px;">End of requests</span>`
        : "";
    }
    updateApprovalsSummaryStrip(approvalsTotalCount);
    setupApprovalsObserver();
    return;
  }

  approvalsPage = 0;
  approvalsHasMore = true;
  approvalsTotalCount = 0;

  if (activeContent) activeContent.innerHTML = getApprovalsSkeletonHTML(3);
  if (sentinel) {
    sentinel.style.display = "flex";
    sentinel.innerHTML = "";
  }

  setupApprovalsObserver();
  loadApprovalsBatch(true);
}

// --- CARD RENDERER ---
function buildApprovalCardHTML(req, searchQuery) {
  const formatApprovalField = (
    oldVal,
    newVal,
    prefix = "",
    fallback = "N/A",
    linkTargets = null,
  ) => {
    const rawOld =
      oldVal === undefined || oldVal === null ? "" : String(oldVal).trim();
    const rawNew =
      newVal === undefined || newVal === null ? "" : String(newVal).trim();
    const sOld = rawOld === "" ? fallback : rawOld;
    const sNew = rawNew === "" ? fallback : rawNew;

    const oldHighlighted = highlightApprovalText(sOld, searchQuery);
    const newHighlighted = highlightApprovalText(sNew, searchQuery);

    const oldHtml = linkTargets
      ? renderDirectoryLink(
          sOld,
          linkTargets.oldOffice,
          linkTargets.oldPin,
          "is-muted",
          oldHighlighted,
        )
      : oldHighlighted;

    const newHtml = linkTargets
      ? renderDirectoryLink(
          sNew,
          linkTargets.newOffice,
          linkTargets.newPin,
          "",
          newHighlighted,
        )
      : newHighlighted;

    if (sOld !== sNew) {
      return `<div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">${prefix} <span style="text-decoration:line-through; color:var(--text-light);">${oldHtml}</span> âž¡ï¸ <b>${newHtml}</b></div>`;
    }
    return `<div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">${prefix} ${newHtml}</div>`;
  };

  let changesHtml = "";

  if (req.action_type === "ADD") {
    const badge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#dcfce7;color:#166534; padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 12px; margin-bottom: 8px;">âž• ADD</span>`;
    const prop = req.proposed_data || {};
    const addOff = prop.Office || prop.office || "";
    const addPin = prop.Pin ?? prop.pin ?? "";
    const addOffLabel = String(addOff).trim() || "Unknown";
    const addPinLabel = String(addPin).trim() || "N/A";

    changesHtml = `
      ${badge}
      <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;"><b>${highlightApprovalText(
        prop.Address || prop.address || "Unknown",
        searchQuery,
      )}</b></div>
      <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">ðŸ¢ ${renderDirectoryLink(
        addOffLabel,
        addOff,
        addPin,
        "",
        highlightApprovalText(addOffLabel, searchQuery),
      )}</div>
      <div>ðŸ“ ${renderDirectoryLink(
        addPinLabel,
        addOff,
        addPin,
        "",
        highlightApprovalText(addPinLabel, searchQuery),
      )}</div>
    `;
  } else if (req.action_type === "EDIT") {
    const badge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#fef9c3;color:#854d0e; padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 12px; margin-bottom: 8px;">âœï¸ EDIT</span>`;
    const oldD = req.original_data || {
      Address: req.po_address,
      Office: req.po_office,
      Pin: req.po_pin,
    };
    const newD = req.proposed_data || {};
    const oldOff = oldD.Office || oldD.office || "";
    const newOff = newD.Office || newD.office || "";
    const oldPin = oldD.Pin ?? oldD.pin ?? "";
    const newPin = newD.Pin ?? newD.pin ?? "";

    const linkTargets = {
      oldOffice: oldOff,
      newOffice: newOff,
      oldPin: oldPin,
      newPin: newPin,
    };

    changesHtml =
      badge +
      formatApprovalField(
        oldD.Address || oldD.address,
        newD.Address || newD.address,
        "ðŸ“®",
        "Unknown Address",
      ) +
      formatApprovalField(oldOff, newOff, "ðŸ¢", "Unknown Office", linkTargets) +
      formatApprovalField(oldPin, newPin, "ðŸ“", "N/A", linkTargets);
  } else if (req.action_type === "DELETE") {
    const badge = `<span class="badge" style="background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b; padding: 4px 8px; font-size: 12px; margin-bottom: 8px;">ðŸ—‘ï¸ DELETE</span>`;
    const fallbackOld = req.original_data || {};
    const delAddr = req.po_address || fallbackOld.Address || "Unknown";
    const delOff = req.po_office || fallbackOld.Office || "";
    const delPin = req.po_pin ?? fallbackOld.Pin ?? "";
    const delOffLabel = String(delOff).trim() || "Unknown";
    const delPinLabel = String(delPin).trim() || "N/A";

    changesHtml = `
      ${badge}
      <div style="text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--text-light); margin-bottom: 4px;"><b>${highlightApprovalText(
        delAddr,
        searchQuery,
      )}</b></div>
      <div style="text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--text-light); margin-bottom: 4px;">ðŸ¢ ${renderDirectoryLink(
        delOffLabel,
        delOff,
        delPin,
        "is-muted",
        highlightApprovalText(delOffLabel, searchQuery),
      )}</div>
      <div style="text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--text-light);">ðŸ“ ${renderDirectoryLink(
        delPinLabel,
        delOff,
        delPin,
        "is-muted",
        highlightApprovalText(delPinLabel, searchQuery),
      )}</div>
    `;
  }

  const voteCount = Number(req.vote_count || 0);
  const dateStr = new Date(req.created_at).toLocaleString("en-IN", {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  });

  const requesterName = req.requester_name || "Unknown User";
  const requesterId = req.requester_emp_id || "No ID";

  let approverDetailsHtml = "";
  if (req.status === "APPROVED" && req.finalizer_id && req.finalizer_name) {
    const approverLink = renderUserProfileLink(
      req.finalizer_id,
      req.finalizer_name,
    );
    const finEmp = escapeHTML(req.finalizer_emp_id || "No ID");
    if (req.is_admin_direct) {
      approverDetailsHtml = `<div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--success-text); margin-top: 8px; padding-top: 8px; border-top: 1px dashed var(--border);">âš¡ <b>Admin Direct</b> (Applied by: ${approverLink} - ${finEmp})</div>`;
    } else if (req.approved_by_admin) {
      approverDetailsHtml = `<div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--success-text); margin-top: 8px; padding-top: 8px; border-top: 1px dashed var(--border);">âœ… <b>Admin Approved</b> (Approved by: ${approverLink} - ${finEmp})</div>`;
    } else {
      approverDetailsHtml = `<div style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--primary); margin-top: 8px; padding-top: 8px; border-top: 1px dashed var(--border);">ðŸ‘¥ <b>Community Approved</b> (Final vote by: ${approverLink} - ${finEmp})</div>`;
    }
  }

  let actionBarHtml = "";
  if (req.status === "PENDING") {
    const isOwnRequest =
      currentUserId && String(req.requested_by) === String(currentUserId);
    const hasAlreadyVoted = Boolean(req.has_user_voted);

    actionBarHtml = `
      <div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-top: 14px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
        ${
          isOwnRequest
            ? `<span style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-light); font-weight: 600; margin-right: auto;">â³ Your request (awaiting votes)</span>
               <button onclick="deleteOwnRequest('${req.id}')" class="dm-btn dm-btn-danger-soft dm-btn-sm">ðŸ—‘ï¸ Delete Request</button>`
            : ""
        }
        ${
          isCurrentUserAdmin && !isOwnRequest
            ? `<button onclick="rejectRequest('${req.id}')" class="dm-btn dm-btn-danger-soft dm-btn-sm">ðŸ‘Ž Reject</button>`
            : ""
        }
        ${
          !isOwnRequest && !hasAlreadyVoted
            ? `<button onclick="approveRequest('${req.id}')" class="dm-btn dm-btn-primary dm-btn-sm">ðŸ‘ Approve</button>`
            : ""
        }
        ${
          !isOwnRequest && hasAlreadyVoted
            ? `<span style="font-size: 13px; color: var(--success-text); font-weight: 700; padding: 6px 12px; background: var(--success-bg); border: 1px solid var(--success-border); border-radius: 10px;">âœ“ You Voted</span>`
            : ""
        }
      </div>`;
  } else if (req.status === "APPROVED") {
    actionBarHtml = `<div style="text-align: right; color: var(--success-text); font-weight: 600; font-size: 13px; margin-top: 12px;">âœ… Request Approved & Merged</div>`;
  } else if (req.status === "REJECTED") {
    let rejecterText = "";
    if (req.finalizer_id && req.finalizer_name) {
      const rejLink = renderUserProfileLink(
        req.finalizer_id,
        req.finalizer_name,
      );
      rejecterText = ` (Rejected by: ${rejLink} - ${escapeHTML(
        req.finalizer_emp_id || "No ID",
      )})`;
    }
    actionBarHtml = `<div style="text-align: right; color: #b91c1c; font-weight: 600; font-size: 13px; margin-top: 12px;">âŒ Request Rejected${rejecterText}</div>`;
  }

  return `
    <div id="approval-card-${req.id}" class="approval-card ${
      req.action_type === "DELETE" ? "is-delete" : ""
    }">
      <div style="font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 12px; color: var(--text); line-height: 1.4;">${changesHtml}</div>
      <div class="approval-meta-box">
        <div>ðŸ‘¤ Requested by: ${renderUserProfileLink(
          req.requested_by,
          requesterName,
        )} (${escapeHTML(requesterId)})</div>
        <div>ðŸ“… ${dateStr}</div>
        ${
          req.status === "PENDING"
            ? `<div style="margin-top: 4px;">ðŸ—³ï¸ Community Votes: <b style="color: var(--primary);">${voteCount} / 5</b></div>`
            : ""
        }
        ${approverDetailsHtml}
      </div>
      ${actionBarHtml}
    </div>`;
}

// --- SERVER-SIDE RPC BATCH LOADER ---
async function loadApprovalsBatch(forceStart = false) {
  const activeTab = currentApprovalTab;
  const cache = approvalsCache[activeTab];
  const isFiltered = hasActiveApprovalFilters(activeTab);

  if (!forceStart && (isApprovalsLoading || !approvalsHasMore)) return;

  const thisSeq = ++approvalsRequestSeq;
  isApprovalsLoading = true;

  const renderCachedApprovalsFallback = () => {
    const contentEl = document.getElementById("approvals-content");
    const sentinelEl = document.getElementById("approvals-sentinel");

    if (isFiltered) {
      showOfflineToast(
        "Connect to internet to search & filter approvals.",
        true,
      );
      if (contentEl) {
        contentEl.innerHTML = `
          <div class="list-empty" style="gap: 10px;">
            <span>ðŸ“¡ You are offline. Connect to the internet to search & filter approvals.</span>
            <button type="button" class="dm-btn dm-btn-secondary dm-btn-sm" onclick="resetApprovalsFilters()">Show Cached View</button>
          </div>`;
      }
      if (sentinelEl) sentinelEl.style.display = "none";
    } else {
      showOfflineToast("Showing cached approvals.", false);
      const savedHtml = localStorage.getItem(
        "dakmitra_approvals_cache_" + activeTab,
      );
      if (contentEl) {
        contentEl.innerHTML =
          savedHtml ||
          `<div class="list-empty">ðŸ“´ No internet connection and no cached ${activeTab} approvals saved yet.</div>`;
      }
      if (sentinelEl) {
        sentinelEl.style.display = "flex";
        sentinelEl.innerHTML = `<span style="color: var(--text-light); font-size: 13px;">ðŸ“´ Offline cached view</span>`;
      }
    }
    approvalsHasMore = false;
    isApprovalsLoading = false;
  };

  if (!navigator.onLine) {
    renderCachedApprovalsFallback();
    return;
  }

  const displayArea = document.getElementById("approvals-display-area");
  let contentDiv = document.getElementById("approvals-content");
  let sentinel = document.getElementById("approvals-sentinel");

  if (!contentDiv && displayArea) {
    displayArea.innerHTML = `
      <div id="approvals-content" style="padding: 4px;">
        ${getApprovalsSkeletonHTML(3)}
      </div>
      <div id="approvals-sentinel" style="min-height: 54px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 10px;"></div>
    `;
    contentDiv = document.getElementById("approvals-content");
    sentinel = document.getElementById("approvals-sentinel");
    setupApprovalsObserver();
  }

  const pageToFetch = approvalsPage;

  if (pageToFetch === 0) {
    if (contentDiv && !contentDiv.querySelector(".approval-skeleton-card")) {
      contentDiv.innerHTML = getApprovalsSkeletonHTML(3);
    }
    if (sentinel) {
      sentinel.style.display = "flex";
      sentinel.innerHTML = "";
    }
  } else if (sentinel) {
    sentinel.style.display = "flex";
    sentinel.innerHTML = `
      <div class="approvals-loader-pill">
        <div class="approvals-spinner"></div>
        <span>Fetching more requests...</span>
      </div>`;
  }

  try {
    await ensureSupabaseReady();

    if (!currentUserId) {
      const {
        data: { session },
      } = await supabaseClient.auth.getSession();
      currentUserId = session?.user?.id || null;
    }

    const { from: dateFromIso, to: dateToIso } = computeApprovalsDateBounds();
    const actionTypesParam =
      approvalsFilters.actionTypes.size > 0
        ? Array.from(approvalsFilters.actionTypes)
        : null;

    const rpcParams = {
      p_tab: activeTab,
      p_search: approvalsFilters.search.trim() || null,
      p_action_types: actionTypesParam,
      p_user_id: approvalsFilters.userId || null,
      p_user_role: approvalsFilters.userRole || "requested",
      p_date_from: dateFromIso,
      p_date_to: dateToIso,
      p_office: approvalsFilters.office.trim() || null,
      p_pin: approvalsFilters.pin.trim() || null,
      p_pending_scope:
        activeTab === "pending" ? approvalsFilters.pendingScope : "all",
      p_vote_progress:
        activeTab === "pending" ? approvalsFilters.voteProgress : "all",
      p_resolved_status:
        activeTab === "resolved" ? approvalsFilters.resolvedStatus : "all",
      p_approval_source:
        activeTab === "resolved" ? approvalsFilters.approvalSource : "all",
      p_only_mine:
        activeTab === "resolved" ? approvalsFilters.resolvedOnlyMine : false,
      p_sort: approvalsFilters.sort || "newest",
      p_limit: APPROVALS_PAGE_SIZE,
      p_offset: pageToFetch * APPROVALS_PAGE_SIZE,
    };

    const { data, error } = await supabaseClient.rpc(
      "search_edit_requests",
      rpcParams,
    );
    if (error) throw error;

    // Discard stale responses from superseded searches/filters
    if (thisSeq !== approvalsRequestSeq || currentApprovalTab !== activeTab) {
      return;
    }

    const rows = Array.isArray(data) ? data : [];
    const totalCount = rows.length > 0 ? Number(rows[0].total_count || 0) : 0;
    approvalsTotalCount = totalCount;
    updateApprovalsSummaryStrip(totalCount);

    if (rows.length === 0) {
      approvalsHasMore = false;
      if (pageToFetch === 0) {
        const emptyHtml = isFiltered
          ? `<div class="list-empty" style="gap: 10px;">
               <span>No ${activeTab} requests match your active filters.</span>
               <button type="button" class="dm-btn dm-btn-secondary dm-btn-sm" onclick="resetApprovalsFilters()">Reset Filters</button>
             </div>`
          : `<div class="list-empty">${
              activeTab === "pending"
                ? "No pending approvals! ðŸŽ‰"
                : "No resolved requests yet."
            }</div>`;

        if (contentDiv) contentDiv.innerHTML = emptyHtml;

        if (!isFiltered) {
          cache.html = emptyHtml;
          cache.page = 1;
          cache.initialHasMore = false;
          cache.totalCount = 0;
          cache.loaded = true;
        }
      }
      if (sentinel) sentinel.style.display = "none";
      isApprovalsLoading = false;
      return;
    }

    const fetchedSoFar = pageToFetch * APPROVALS_PAGE_SIZE + rows.length;
    if (rows.length < APPROVALS_PAGE_SIZE || fetchedSoFar >= totalCount) {
      approvalsHasMore = false;
      if (sentinel) {
        sentinel.style.display = "flex";
        sentinel.innerHTML = `<span style="color: var(--text-light); font-size: 13px;">End of requests</span>`;
      }
    } else if (sentinel) {
      sentinel.style.display = "flex";
      sentinel.innerHTML = "";
    }

    const searchQ = approvalsFilters.search.trim();
    const html = rows
      .map((req) => buildApprovalCardHTML(req, searchQ))
      .join("");

    if (pageToFetch === 0) {
      if (contentDiv) {
        contentDiv.classList.remove("approvals-smooth-in");
        contentDiv.innerHTML = html;
        void contentDiv.offsetWidth;
        contentDiv.classList.add("approvals-smooth-in");
      }
      approvalsPage = 1;

      // Only cache unfiltered default view so offline snapshot stays clean
      if (!isFiltered) {
        cache.html = html;
        cache.loaded = true;
        cache.initialHasMore = approvalsHasMore;
        cache.totalCount = totalCount;
        cache.page = 1;
        localStorage.setItem("dakmitra_approvals_cache_" + activeTab, html);
      }
    } else {
      if (contentDiv) contentDiv.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", html);
      approvalsPage++;
    }

    isApprovalsLoading = false;
  } catch (err) {
    if (thisSeq !== approvalsRequestSeq) return;
    if (isNetworkError(err)) {
      renderCachedApprovalsFallback();
      return;
    }
    isApprovalsLoading = false;
    if (contentDiv) {
      contentDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="list-empty" style="color: red;">Error loading requests: ${escapeHTML(
        err.message,
      )}</div>`;
    }
  }
}

function setupApprovalsObserver() {
  const sentinel = document.getElementById("approvals-sentinel");
  const displayArea = document.getElementById("approvals-display-area");

  if (approvalsObserver) approvalsObserver.disconnect();

  approvalsObserver = new IntersectionObserver(
    (entries) => {
      if (
        entries[0].isIntersecting &&
        approvalsHasMore &&
        !isApprovalsLoading
      ) {
        loadApprovalsBatch(false);
      }
    },
    { root: displayArea, rootMargin: "200px", threshold: 0.1 },
  );

  if (sentinel) approvalsObserver.observe(sentinel);
}

async function approveRequest(reqId) {
  if (!(await ensureOnline("Connect to internet to save changes."))) return;
  const card = document.getElementById("approval-card-" + reqId);
  if (card) card.style.display = "none";

  try {
    const { data, error } = await supabaseClient.rpc("submit_approval", {
      req_id: reqId,
    });
    if (error) throw error;

    if (data === "APPROVED") {
      showToast("âœ… Request approved and executed!");
      await fetchAllPostOffices(true);
    } else {
      showToast("ðŸ‘ Vote recorded!");
    }
    invalidateApprovalsCache();
    switchApprovalTab(currentApprovalTab, true);
  } catch (err) {
    if (card) card.style.display = "block";
    alert(err.message);
  }
}

async function rejectRequest(reqId) {
  showCustomConfirm(
    "Reject Request?",
    "Are you sure you want to reject and close this community request?",
    "Reject",
    async () => {
      if (!(await ensureOnline("Connect to internet to save changes."))) return;
      const card = document.getElementById("approval-card-" + reqId);
      if (card) card.style.display = "none";

      try {
        const { error } = await supabaseClient.rpc("reject_request", {
          req_id: reqId,
        });
        if (error) throw error;
        showToast("âŒ Request rejected and closed.");
        invalidateApprovalsCache();
        switchApprovalTab(currentApprovalTab, true);
      } catch (err) {
        if (card) card.style.display = "block";
        alert(err.message);
      }
    },
  );
}

async function deleteOwnRequest(reqId) {
  showCustomConfirm(
    "Delete Your Request?",
    "Are you sure you want to withdraw and delete this pending request?",
    "Delete Request",
    async () => {
      if (!(await ensureOnline("Connect to internet to save changes."))) return;
      const card = document.getElementById("approval-card-" + reqId);
      if (card) card.style.display = "none";

      try {
        const { error } = await supabaseClient
          .from("edit_requests")
          .delete()
          .eq("id", reqId)
          .eq("status", "PENDING");

        if (error) throw error;

        showToast("ðŸ—‘ï¸ Your request has been deleted.");
        invalidateApprovalsCache();
        switchApprovalTab(currentApprovalTab, true);
      } catch (err) {
        if (card) card.style.display = "block";
        alert("Error deleting request: " + err.message);
      }
    },
  );
}


// ===== FILE: 10-settings-boot.js =====
// =========================================================
// 10-SETTINGS-BOOT.JS â€” Settings, Admin UI, Export & App Boot
// =========================================================

// --- SETTINGS & PREFERENCES ---
function saveDefaultView(mode) {
  localStorage.setItem("dakmitra_default_view", mode);
  setViewMode(mode);
  showToast("ðŸ‘ï¸ Default view saved!");
}

function saveExportMode(mode) {
  localStorage.setItem("dakmitra_export_mode", mode);
  applyExportMode(mode);
  showToast("âš™ï¸ Export button preference saved!");
}

function applyExportMode(mode) {
  const exportGroup = document.getElementById("export-toolbar-group");
  if (exportGroup) {
    exportGroup.style.display = mode === "hide" ? "none" : "flex";
  }
}

function clearAppCache() {
  showCustomConfirm(
    "Clear Cache?",
    "This will reset your recent searches and preferences. You will not lose your database records.",
    "Clear Cache",
    () => {
      localStorage.removeItem(HISTORY_KEY);
      localStorage.removeItem("dakmitra_default_view");
      localStorage.removeItem("dakmitra_offline_db");
      localStorage.removeItem(OFFICE_STAFF_CACHE_KEY);
      cachedOfficeStaff = [];
      officeStaffByKeyMap.clear();
      localStorage.removeItem("dakmitra_members_cache");
      localStorage.removeItem("dakmitra_feedback_cache");
      localStorage.removeItem("dakmitra_learning_leaderboard_cache");
      if (typeof invalidateFeedbackCache === "function") {
        invalidateFeedbackCache();
      }
      showToast("ðŸ§¹ Cache cleared successfully!");
      loadSearchHistory();
      invalidateApprovalsCache();
    },
  );
}

async function syncDatabase() {
  if (!(await ensureOnline("Connect to internet to sync database."))) return;
  const syncBtn = document.getElementById("sync-db-btn");
  if (!syncBtn) return;

  setButtonState(syncBtn, "loading", "Syncing...");
  const success = await fetchAllPostOffices(true);

  if (success) {
    setButtonState(syncBtn, "success", "Synced");
    showToast("ðŸ”„ Database synced successfully!");
    invalidateApprovalsCache();
    setTimeout(() => setButtonState(syncBtn, "idle"), 1800);
  } else {
    setButtonState(syncBtn, "idle");
    showToast("âš ï¸ Sync failed. Try again later.");
  }
}

// --- ADMIN ROLE CHECK & UI ---
async function checkIfAdmin(userId) {
  try {
    const { data } = await supabaseClient
      .from("admins")
      .select("id")
      .eq("id", userId)
      .maybeSingle();

    return !!data;
  } catch (err) {
    return false;
  }
}

async function applyAdminUI() {
  const adminOnlyExcelCard = document.getElementById("admin-excel-upload");
  if (!adminOnlyExcelCard) return;
  adminOnlyExcelCard.style.display = isCurrentUserAdmin ? "block" : "none";
}

// --- FULL DATABASE EXCEL EXPORT ---
async function exportFullTableToExcel(includeDeleted = true) {
  try {
    await ensureXLSXLoaded();
  } catch (e) {
    return showOfflineToast("Excel library needs internet the first time.");
  }
  const btn = document.getElementById("export-excel-btn");
  if (!supabaseClient || !window.XLSX) {
    return showToast("âš ï¸ Exporter still loading. Try again in a moment.");
  }

  const exportCachedFallback = () => {
    if (!cachedPostOffices || cachedPostOffices.length === 0) {
      return showOfflineToast("No cached data available to export.");
    }
    const formattedRows = cachedPostOffices.map((row) => ({
      Address: row.Address,
      Office: row.Office,
      Pin: row.Pin ?? "",
      Priority: Number(row.priority) >= 1 ? 1 : 0,
      Status: "Active (Cached)",
    }));
    const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(formattedRows);
    const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "Post Offices");
    XLSX.writeFile(wb, "DakMitra_Cached_Database.xlsx");
    return showOfflineToast("Exported cached database instead.");
  };

  if (!navigator.onLine) {
    return exportCachedFallback();
  }

  const originalText = btn ? btn.textContent : "Export";
  if (btn) {
    btn.textContent = "Exporting...";
    btn.disabled = true;
  }
  showToast("â³ Preparing Excel export...");

  try {
    let allRows = [];
    let from = 0;
    const batchSize = 1000;

    while (true) {
      let query = supabaseClient
        .from("post_offices")
        .select("Address, Office, Pin, priority, is_deleted")
        .order("Office", { ascending: true })
        .range(from, from + batchSize - 1);

      if (!includeDeleted) {
        query = query.eq("is_deleted", false);
      }

      const { data, error } = await query;
      if (error) throw error;
      if (!data || data.length === 0) break;

      allRows = allRows.concat(data);
      if (data.length < batchSize) break;
      from += batchSize;
    }

    if (allRows.length === 0) {
      showToast("No records found to export.");
      return;
    }

    const formattedRows = allRows.map((row) => ({
      Address: row.Address,
      Office: row.Office,
      Pin: row.Pin ?? "",
      Priority: Number(row.priority) >= 1 ? 1 : 0,
      Status: row.is_deleted ? "Deleted" : "Active",
    }));

    const worksheet = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(formattedRows);
    worksheet["!cols"] = [
      { wch: 28 },
      { wch: 22 },
      { wch: 12 },
      { wch: 10 },
      { wch: 12 },
    ];

    const workbook = XLSX.utils.book_new();
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, worksheet, "Post Offices");
    XLSX.writeFile(workbook, "DakMitra_Database_With_History.xlsx");

    showToast(`ðŸ“¥ Exported ${formattedRows.length} rows to Excel!`);
  } catch (err) {
    if (isNetworkError(err)) {
      exportCachedFallback();
    } else {
      alert("Excel export failed: " + err.message);
    }
  } finally {
    if (btn) {
      btn.textContent = originalText;
      btn.disabled = false;
    }
  }
}

// --- RESTORE SAVED DISPLAY PREFERENCES ---
(function initSavedPreferences() {
  const savedView = localStorage.getItem("dakmitra_default_view") || "list";
  const defaultViewSelect = document.getElementById("default-view-select");
  if (defaultViewSelect) defaultViewSelect.value = savedView;
  setViewMode(savedView, false);

  const savedExportMode =
    localStorage.getItem("dakmitra_export_mode") || "show";
  const exportModeSelect = document.getElementById("export-mode-select");
  if (exportModeSelect) exportModeSelect.value = savedExportMode;
  applyExportMode(savedExportMode);
})();

// --- 0ms INSTANT BOOT & SESSION SYNC ---
function getInstantLocalSession() {
  try {
    for (let i = 0; i < localStorage.length; i++) {
      const key = localStorage.key(i);
      if (key && key.startsWith("sb-") && key.endsWith("-auth-token")) {
        const raw = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key));
        const session = raw?.session || raw;
        if (session && session.user) return session;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return null;
}

function bootAppInstantly() {
  const authView = document.getElementById("auth-view");
  const dashboardView = document.getElementById("dashboard-view");
  const splashScreen = document.getElementById("splash-screen");

  const localSession = getInstantLocalSession();

  if (localSession) {
    currentUserId = localSession.user.id;
    authView.classList.add("hidden");
    dashboardView.classList.remove("hidden");

    const userEmail = localSession.user.email || "user@example.com";
    let profileData = null;
    try {
      profileData = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("dakmitra_cached_profile"));
    } catch (e) {}

    renderUserProfileUI(profileData, userEmail);

    switchDashboardView("search");

    if (typeof loadOfficeStaffFromCache === "function") {
      loadOfficeStaffFromCache();
    }

    const cachedDataString = localStorage.getItem("dakmitra_offline_db");
    if (cachedDataString) {
      try {
        cachedPostOffices = JSON.parse(cachedDataString);
        if (typeof buildSearchIndex === "function") buildSearchIndex();
        document.getElementById("stat-addresses").textContent =
          cachedPostOffices.length;
        document.getElementById("stat-offices").textContent = new Set(
          cachedPostOffices.map((o) => o.Office),
        ).size;
        currentFilteredData = cachedPostOffices
          .map((item) => ({
            item,
            matches: [],
          }))
          .sort((a, b) => compareByPriorityOnly(a.item, b.item));
        renderResults(false);
        if (typeof updateSidebarLearnBadge === "function") {
          updateSidebarLearnBadge();
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }
  } else {
    currentUserId = null;
    authView.classList.remove("hidden");
    dashboardView.classList.add("hidden");
  }

  loadSearchHistory();

  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    splashScreen.classList.add("hidden-splash");
  });
}

async function checkUserSession() {
  if (!navigator.onLine) {
    wasOffline = true;
  }

  initSupabaseClient();

  if (cachedPostOffices.length > 0 && window.Fuse && !fuse) {
    initializeSearchAndRender();
  }

  let session = null;
  if (supabaseClient) {
    const { data } = await supabaseClient.auth.getSession();
    session = data?.session || getInstantLocalSession();
  } else {
    session = getInstantLocalSession();
  }

  const authView = document.getElementById("auth-view");
  const dashboardView = document.getElementById("dashboard-view");
  const splashScreen = document.getElementById("splash-screen");

  if (session) {
    currentUserId = session.user.id;
    authView.classList.add("hidden");
    dashboardView.classList.remove("hidden");
    hideSplashScreen();
    splashScreen.classList.add("hidden-splash");

    const userEmail = session.user.email || "user@example.com";

    let cachedProf = null;
    try {
      cachedProf = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("dakmitra_cached_profile"));
    } catch (e) {}

    if (cachedProf) {
      if (typeof cachedProf.is_admin === "boolean") {
        isCurrentUserAdmin = cachedProf.is_admin;
        applyAdminUI();
      }
      renderUserProfileUI(cachedProf, userEmail);
    }

    if (navigator.onLine && supabaseClient) {
      Promise.all([
        Promise.resolve(checkIfAdmin(session.user.id)).catch(
          () => isCurrentUserAdmin,
        ),
        Promise.resolve(
          supabaseClient
            .from("profiles")
            .select(
              "name, designation, employee_id, mobile, avatar_url, show_mobile",
            )
            .eq("id", session.user.id)
            .single(),
        ).catch(() => ({ data: null })),
      ]).then(([isAdmin, profRes]) => {
        isCurrentUserAdmin = isAdmin;
        applyAdminUI();

        const prof = profRes?.data;
        let existing = {};
        try {
          existing =
            JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("dakmitra_cached_profile")) || {};
        } catch (e) {}

        const merged = {
          ...existing,
          ...(prof || {}),
          email: userEmail,
          is_admin: isAdmin,
        };
        localStorage.setItem("dakmitra_cached_profile", JSON.stringify(merged));
        if (prof) renderUserProfileUI(merged, userEmail);
      });

      initRealtimeSync();
      if (typeof refreshFeedbackOpenCount === "function") {
        refreshFeedbackOpenCount();
      }
      if (typeof syncLearningProgressInBackground === "function") {
        syncLearningProgressInBackground();
      }
    }

    fetchAllPostOffices();
  } else {
    currentUserId = null;
    authView.classList.remove("hidden");
    dashboardView.classList.add("hidden");
    splashScreen.classList.add("hidden-splash");
  }
}

// --- BOOTSTRAP ---
bootAppInstantly();

window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  initSupabaseClient();
  checkEmailVerificationRedirect();
  checkUserSession();
});
